## Upstream changes by commit

### `75f76a8a3744` — fix(serve): Inkling takes the shared Chat-settings level and the config's reasoning_effort like every model (#7035)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/MODELS.md` (+1/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+20/-7)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_config_effort.py` (+87/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_control/inkling_family.py` (+0/-3)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
effort_default() returned None for Inkling (from the time it could not think), so a config's
reasoning_effort and the level shared with other apps were skipped; since 0.2.21 a request with no level
thinks at low, so the ignored config mattered. The order is now the same as for other models: the request's
level, then the shared Chat settings, then the config, and only when none is set the Inkling default (low,
STRATA_INKLING_EFFORT), which 0.2.21 chose on purpose and stays.

The start-up line (thinking_level_line) names only a level that is used; for Inkling with no
reasoning_effort in the config it says the default is in use. The unused constants in inkling_family
(REASONING_EFFORTS, DEFAULT_REASONING_EFFORT, HAS_THINKING) that still said "none" are gone.
```

### `e2491d8ef260` — fix(serve): a tool result keeps the name of the call it answers, so Inkling's header is not empty (#7051)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/serve/frontend.py` (+24/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/responses.py` (+3/-2)
  - `terminal/serve/test_tool_result_names.py` (+130/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The front end dropped the call ids before the chat template ran and never copied the function name onto the
result, so Inkling's `<|message_tool|>NAME<|content_text|>` header came out as `<|message_tool|><|content_text|>`
(its own match by tool_call_id could never happen). Each result now takes the name of the call with its id in the
assistant turn before the run of results, before the ids are removed and the results put in call order. A result
whose id matches no call, or two, stays unnamed. Chat completions, the /v1/messages API and the Responses API all
go through it; only Inkling gets the name (the other templates do not read it, so their renders stay as they
were).

Tests: one call, two calls answered in reverse order, two rounds, ambiguous ids, the three APIs, other families
unchanged, against the repo's real Inkling template.
```

### `89ef8456aa97` — fix(serve): the engine's READY wait moves while the engine shows it is working, so a slow disk is not cut off at 15 minutes (#7060)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+3/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+69/-4)
  - `terminal/serve/test_ready_deadline.py` (+170/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
The 900 s READY deadline was one absolute time from the start of the engine. The Terminal launcher already gave
big models more, but the app and `strata serve` did not, so a model read from a slow hard disk failed with
"the engine did not report READY within 900 s" while still loading.

ENGINE_READY_S (config "engine_ready_s", STRATA_ENGINE_READY_S, default 900, 0 = no limit) now counts from the
last sign of life: a new line in the engine's log or more bytes read from disk by the engine process (psutil;
without it the log alone). Each sign moves the deadline to a full period after it. An engine that goes quiet is
still ended after that period. The whole start ends at 2 hours (or four periods if that is longer) whatever it
prints. The override keeps its meaning, the Terminal launcher's size-based value still applies, and the error
now says it counts from the last sign of life and how to change it.

engine_ready_s is documented in docs/DETAILS.md (config keys and troubleshooting).

Tests: the deadline with a fake clock (silent, log growth, disk reads, ceiling, failing probe) and live starts of a
fake engine (slow but writing: not ended; quiet: ended after the period).
```

### `6c3f3fbb4fbc` — feat(serve): the conversation cache grows with the PC's RAM instead of stopping at 8 GiB, through the engine's own option (#7044)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+1/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+11/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/test_conversation_cache_budget.py` (+92/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_control/conversation_cache_size.py` (+102/-0)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_conversation_cache_size.py` (+122/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
The engine sizes the RAM for parked conversations itself when --conversation-cache-mib is not given: 40% of the
RAM free once the model is loaded, at most 8192 MiB. One conversation above about 490K tokens never fits, whatever
the RAM, so a long agent session read its whole prompt again every turn. The owner decided the budget should follow
the PC's RAM.

No engine change: at each start of an engine program that has --conversation-cache-mib (checked in the binary, as
for --conversation-cache-min-tokens), engine_args adds it from the RAM Control sees before the model loads:
40% of (free RAM - the model file - 4 GiB - the RAM floor, 2560 MiB by default), but at most a quarter of the PC's
RAM and 128 GiB, in steps of 256 MiB, and only when that is above 8192 MiB. Everything else is left to the engine
as before: a PC of 32 GiB or less, a model that fills the RAM, an unreadable RAM figure (inside a container the
memory limit counts), the options the person set (--conversation-cache-mib including 0, --prompt-cache 0,
--conversation-cache-slots 0), Hy4 and Inkling, and STRATA_NO_AUTO_CONVERSATION_CACHE=1. The engine's own gate
still refuses a park that would leave less than the floor free. The size is added after the min-tokens default so
that default is not pulled in by it. One line at the start says what was set.

Documented in docs/DETAILS.md. Tests: the sizing (big PC, quarter of the RAM, ceiling, never below the engine's
8 GiB, growth with the RAM), the arguments (what is added, what is never touched) and engine_args.
```

### `d32eb528d196` — fix(serve): Inkling pictures on the CPU reader: scaled to 0.8 MP, and a request with pictures is never ended by the silence watchdog (#7043)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+1/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/inkling_kind.py` (+117/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+35/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_image_cap.py` (+208/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
strata-inkling reads a picture with its GPU encoder where it has one (HIP, SYCL, Metal). CUDA has none, so the engine falls
back to a CPU encoder that only downscales above 4096 x 4096 and says nothing while it works. Measured on the owner PC (6 cores,
12 threads, nothing else running, one picture, prompt to first token): 0.25 MP 15 s, 0.5 MP 32 s, 0.75 MP 55 s, 1.0 MP 74 s,
2.07 MP 334 s, 3.0 MP 743 s (about the square of the pixels from 2 MP up). The engine has no option for any of it, so Control
does two things where the reader is the CPU (the run config's backend cuda or cpu):

- It scales a picture down before it writes the file the engine reads (Pillow, same proportions, LANCZOS; JPEG stays JPEG,
  else PNG) to at most 0.8 MP, where the measured reader takes about a minute. A picture at or under the cap, a 1024 x 768 one for
  instance, is passed on byte for byte; a 1920 x 1080 screenshot becomes about 1190 x 670. One line in the server window names
  every picture that was scaled.
- A request with pictures gets a silence allowance in StrataEngine.generate: 3 times the measured time of its pictures (the
  measured table, read on a log-log line, the square beyond 3 MP) plus a minute, at most 3 hours, and never less than the
  server's own engine_silence_s (the person's larger value stays). So a big picture, with the cap raised or off, is slow and
  answered, not killed. One line says how long the request may stay silent.

Setting: "inkling_image_max_mp" in the run config (megapixels; 0 turns the cap off; a number above 0 sets it on every backend,
HIP, SYCL and Metal included, which have no default cap) or STRATA_INKLING_IMAGE_MAX_MP in the environment (the config wins).
A value that is not a number >= 0 stops the start with a message naming the key. Documented in docs/DETAILS.md.

Tests: the setting by backend, scaling (proportions, near the cap, JPEG / PNG / RGBA / palette, unreadable passed on, a sliver),
a picture under the cap written to the engine's folder unchanged, the allowance (follows the pixels, covers the measured time
with the margin, unmeasurable pictures count as the cap), and live starts of a fake engine that stays silent for 2.5 s: ended
without the allowance, answered with it, the person's larger silence kept.
```

### `8111e580d326` — Control 0.1.68.1 / Terminal 0.2.21.2: version bump for the second Control-only update (app stays 0.2.21, engine packages stay 0.1.67)

- Date: 2026-11-24
- Author: Niko1221

  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_tui2_023_versions.py` (+5/-4)
  - `tools/packaging/README.md` (+1/-1)
  - `tools/packaging/UPDATES.md` (+3/-3)

- Related decisions:
  - `cmake-upstream-build`: upstream — Берём version 0.1.70, symbols, sm120/draft-rule tests и dot-x4 policy; fork parity tests сохраняются отдельно.
  - `packaging-fork-notes`: ours — Upstream меняет только version line; fork CI/gap notes сохраняются.
  - `packaging-upstream-version`: upstream — Обновляем version line до 0.2.23 / Control 0.1.70 / engines 0.1.70.

**Original commit message**

```
As 0.2.14.1 did (Control 0.1.60.1): the Terminal says 0.2.21.2 (strata_cli PRODUCT_VERSION), Control is 0.1.68.1 (the CMake
project version, which strata --version and a source checkout read; the Control packages get it from pack-control --version),
and the app, its installers, latest.json and every engine package stay as v0.2.21 shipped them.

Why 0.1.68.1 and not 0.1.69: the feed and the updater compare versions with feed.version_key, which pads to four numbers, so
0.1.68.1 sorts above 0.1.68 (the Control of 0.2.21.1, installed by `strata update` as "available") and below 0.1.69, which stays
free for the next engine release. A 0.1.69 Control would take that engine number (0.1.68 is already skipped as an engine number).

docs/data/release.json names terminal_version 0.2.21.2 and control_version 0.1.68.1 and lists the four 0.1.68.1 Control packages
(the 0.1.67 and 0.1.68 ones stay as rollback targets); the pages release_manifest.py and capability_table.py write, and
tools/packaging/UPDATES.md, follow; test_tui2_023_versions.py pins the new numbers.
```

### `924e59f4d208` — fix(mac): the Qwen Metal engine starts on macOS 15 with the prompt through the decode windows (#7082, #3915)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/MAC.md` (+15/-2)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+21/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_control/mac_fit.py` (+40/-0)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_mac_pre26_metal_env.py` (+233/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
The Qwen Metal engine compiles kernels that include <MetalPerformancePrimitives/...>, a header only macOS 26 ships, and Context::compile() ends the process
(exit 3) when a library does not build. On macOS 15 it stopped at the prompt kernels (a resident Mac: the prompt chain; a streaming Mac: the prompt GEMM that
stream_arena sets up with the arena) or at the matmul layout probe of the decode window.

Read in engine/metal at the 0.1.67 engine: every library that holds an MPP kernel (lib_prompt, lib_prompt_qsa, lib_coop_probe) is compiled behind exactly one of
STRATA_METAL_PROMPT=window (Window::prompt -> pc_setup), STRATA_METAL_PROMPT_GEMM=0 (prompt_gemm_init) and COOP_K4=0 (both coop_k4 probes, m5_layout); the other
MPP files (gdn_tprobe, mm_i8) are test programs only.

Control starts the engine with those three settings on macOS below 26, for each one the person has not set (their own environment and the run config win), and prints
one line saying why. Inkling (supports_metal4) and Hy4 (compile with a fallback) keep their own guards and are not touched. Decode kernels do not change: a GPU
without the M5 layout, which every chip a Mac below macOS 26 can have, answers COOP_K4=0 by itself.

Tests: values per macOS version, person-set values kept, the server's child_env (once-per-version line, Inkling and Hy4 and other platforms untouched), and the
engine-source reading pinned as a test. docs/MAC.md says what macOS 15 gets.
```

### `1e5070f739e4` — fix(serve): the greedy default under --mtp applies to Qwen3.6 only; Flash-Next and Gyro keep the model card's sampling (#3354, #6536, #7070)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+10/-4)
  - `terminal/serve/server_parts/http_api.py` (+12/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/test_model_card_sampling.py` (+23/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
#6536 (0.2.20) made a request that names no sampling run greedy whenever the engine args hold --mtp, for every model, so the MTP drafts are not switched off by
the card's presence penalty. That is true for Qwen3.6 (the #3354 case: its engine drafts only strictly greedy requests). Flash-Next and Gyro (qwen4exp) verify the
drafts of sampled requests, and every Gyro / Qwen setup writes --mtp, so since 0.2.20 their thinking requests ran greedy instead of with temperature 1.0 /
top_p 0.95 / top_k 20 as in 0.2.13 (the #7070 report: Gyro-S solving far fewer coding tasks).

http_api.GREEDY_FOR_DRAFTS names the families that need it (qwen35moe); mtp_greedy_default(arch, has_mtp, *layers) is the one rule the chat route and the /v1
defaults route both read. Where it still applies, the server says so once.

Tests: test_model_card_sampling.py gains the Flash-Next-keeps-the-card case and the rule's table (14 tests in the file, all passing).
```

### `752ac4519d78` — fix(serve): Inkling's thinking budget stays armed after the wrap-up, so a thinking turn the model opens again is capped (#7084)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+22/-4)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_rewrap_7084.py` (+133/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
When the thinking budget is reached the server appends the wrap-up (which ends in <|end_message|>) and used to clear the budget. Inkling-2-Mini then sometimes opened
a SECOND thinking turn, nothing counted it, and it ran to max_tokens (32,000 hidden tokens, finish "length", an empty reply to the client; 2 of 3 runs of one agent
task on a 75K-token prompt). The first-ever budget wrap-up of Inkling is 0.2.21's (#6734); 0.2.20 had no budget at all for Inkling, so this is the failure of the
new feature, not a regression of a working one.

Now, on Inkling only: after a wrap-up the budget is re-armed for a re-opened thinking turn, at most INKLING_REWRAP_BUDGET (512) tokens of it, or the budget when
smaller; the second wrap-up closes the turn AND opens the model's text turn (<|message_model|><|content_text|>, the same opener the empty-reply retry uses), so the
model has to answer; a forced call keeps its own opening. Every other family clears the budget at the wrap-up as before. INKLING_REWRAP_BUDGET = 0 gives 0.2.21's behaviour.

Tests: serve/test_inkling_rewrap_7084.py (a scripted engine that re-opens thinking after the wrap-up: capped, second wrap-up carries the opener, the old behaviour
reproduced with the cap at 0, the other families untouched).
```

### `289fa51dea00` — fix(setup): Auto is the MTP on the canonical Q2_0 pack of an AVX-512 CPU, so a fresh CUDA, HIP or SYCL start no longer ends at "it needs a native pack" (#7090)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/setup.py` (+9/-5)
  - `terminal/strata_control/drafting.py` (+21/-2)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_drafting_canonical_pack_7090.py` (+188/-0)
  - `terminal/tools/test_setup_auto_drafter.py` (+1/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - `setup-kat-huihui`: ours — Upstream добавляет Ornith/custom builds, но не KAT/Huihui pins и per-model own_file/own_hf.
  - `setup-upstream-features`: upstream — Берём Ornith, custom-label, hardware refresh, DFlash2 и setup error hint; KAT/Huihui остаются отдельной ours-строкой.
  - `mcp-upstream-features`: upstream — Берём stale-ok hardware refresh, custom models, Inkling legacy/update metadata; наша own-size строка сохраняется.

**Original commit message**

```
On a CPU with AVX-512 (Zen 4/5, Ice Lake and newer) setup builds Qwen Q2_0 as the canonical pack (experts.bin, no native_experts.txt). Auto still meant DFlash2 on
the card, so setup skipped the 5 GB MTP layer and Control started the engine with --draft dflash2 --draft-fallback none; the engine refuses DFlash2 on a pack that
is not native ("it needs a native pack; there is no MTP to draft instead, so the start ends here"). Since 0.2.16. `--drafting mtp` alone did the same: with the layer
missing, DFlash2 stood in for it.

drafting.canonical_pack(args) reads --pack (experts.bin and no native_experts.txt; an empty or missing folder is not known). choice_of: Auto is the MTP on such a
pack (`canonical=` says so for a pack that is not built yet). decide: DFlash2 is not the stand-in for a missing MTP layer on it either, so the start has no drafter
and the launch's existing safety net (fetch_missing_drafter) fetches the layer. setup.auto_drafter takes canonical= (Q2_0, avx512, family qwen, no downloaded pack),
so a fresh setup fetches the MTP layer; a DFlash2 handed in by the app or --drafter for that pack gets a warning and the MTP layer.

Not changed: Q2_0 on an AVX2 CPU (its pack is native, DFlash2 works), every other set and pack, gfx1201 (AUTO_MTP), the Mac (the Metal engine has its own rules),
DFlash2 asked for by name on a start (the engine says why).

Tests: tests/terminal/test_drafting_canonical_pack_7090.py (both CPU paths, gfx1201, the Mac, named choices, setup's rule), setup wiring test updated. A sweep of 47,040
choice_of / decide rows differs from f-02212 only on canonical-pack rows with Auto or MTP.
```

  - `app/host/src/locales/es.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/fr.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/he.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/ja.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/ko.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/pt.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/ru.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/locales/zh.json` (+4/-0)
  - `app/host/src/screens/Chat.tsx` (+28/-2)
  - `app/host/tests/fix2app-0216-wf1-update-summary.test.mjs` (+2/-2)
  - `app/host/tests/update-resume-7099.test.mjs` (+206/-0)
  - `app/host/tests/update-resume-ui-7099.test.mjs` (+63/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
"Update Strata" pauses the agent first (agent.prepareForUpdate) because an
app install restarts the app. Only a host restart cleared that pause, so a
Control-only update (no new app, no restart) left every chat turn failing with
"Agent work is paused for the Strata update. Restart Strata to resume." The
chat toolbar's "Pause for update" button and /pause-for-update called the same
function and had no way back either.

- agent-bridge: resumeAfterUpdate() clears the in-memory pause, the cached
  preparation and the pause marker file the Harness reads to refuse its tools.
  The saved checkpoint stays on disk. status() reports `paused`. The paused
  error now names Resume agents.
- native-control: the update-strata job calls the new afterUpdate hook when it
  ends for any reason except a finished app install (Control only, already up
  to date, failed, cancelled, pause not confirmed, app installer failed). After
  the app installer took over, the pause stays until the restart.
- server/index.mjs: afterUpdate -> agent.resumeAfterUpdate; new route
  POST /api/chat/resume-after-update, refused (409) while an Update Strata job
  runs.
- Chat toolbar: the button turns into "Resume agents" while the host is
  paused (read from /api/agent/status and kept in step after pause and resume,
  also after a failed preparation); new /resume-agents command. Four new
  sentences in all ten languages.
- The Control-updated summary now says what is true: the host keeps running the
  Control it started with, so quit Strata completely and open it again. After an
  app install the summary no longer tells the person to quit.
- Tests: update-resume-7099 (Control only, up to date, failure, unconfirmed
  pause, cancel, app install keeps the pause, installer failure, other jobs,
  the real bridge and marker, end to end) and update-resume-ui-7099 (status,
  route guard, page wiring, catalogs).
```

### `d86322048138` — App host: only the app's own page may use /api from a browser (#7130)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `.github/SECURITY.md` (+7/-1)
  - `app/host/server/index.mjs` (+15/-7)
  - `app/host/server/request-guard.mjs` (+118/-0)
  - `app/host/src/lib/host-token.ts` (+35/-0)
  - `app/host/src/main.tsx` (+2/-0)
  - `app/host/tests/request-guard.test.mjs` (+193/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The host checked the hostname of a browser Origin but not its port, answered every origin with Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *, never looked at the
Host header and asked for no token on the chat routes. A page served on another localhost port could change a chat's mode and start or approve
turns; a rebound name could read the host's pages and files.

server/request-guard.mjs, applied before routing on the app's own listener:
- Host must name this PC (localhost, 127.x.x.x, [::1], STRATA_ALLOWED_HOSTS) while the host listens on this PC only.
- An Origin on /api must be the host's own page (own port, or the same origin as Host); a development run also accepts Vite on 5199 and
  STRATA_DEV_ORIGINS. "null", empty and unreadable origins are refused.
- Sec-Fetch-Site cross-site and same-site (another localhost port) are refused on /api.
- A change a browser sends to /api (Origin or Sec-Fetch-Site present) needs this launch's token in x-strata-token. The host puts the token in the
  index.html it serves (meta strata-token, only to its own origin) and src/lib/host-token.ts adds it to the page's own /api changes. Scripts,
  the Terminal and the shell send neither header and are not asked.
- CORS: only /v1 answers any origin (local web UIs); /api answers its own page, the static page answers none. index.html is not framable
  by another site.

SECURITY.md describes the rules. Tests: tests/request-guard.test.mjs (real host: other site, other port, null, wrong Host, missing token,
own page still works, preflights, the page's fetch wrapper).
```

### `f36cd62e556b` — fix(inkling-cuda): a peer card's expert budget was read from the wrong card, so a layer split put host experts on a card that cannot compute them (#7093)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda.cuh` (+9/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+133/-10)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
From #7093 by @locafra (the diagnosis and the three-part patch this builds on).

Symptom: Inkling gave silent wrong output (garbage, no error, --selfcheck
passes) on 2 to 4 NVIDIA cards without P2P (every GeForce pair, the default
"layer_split": "auto") whenever the plan had host (RAM) experts. Since engine
0.1.66 (29caf4880).

Cause, two parts:
1. Host experts are computed only on stage 0 (the hybrid branch of the MoE
   step needs cur_stage == 0). On a peer stage a layer with host experts went
   to the plain launch_ink_moe, which reads the expert table as if every entry
   sat in that card's VRAM.
2. How a peer stage got host experts at all: ink_cuda_mem_info(&free, &total,
   dev) ignored `dev` and called cudaMemGetInfo on the CURRENT device. The tier
   step asked for each stage's memory while the primary card was current, so a
   peer stage was budgeted with the primary's free memory after its trunk
   (7.2 GiB on 2 x RTX 3060) instead of its own (10.8 GiB). The auto partition,
   which had measured correctly, planned 12 layers for the peer and the tier
   step then found "no room" for 363 of their experts. (The reporter's
   11,793 -> 8,291 MiB "drop" was this reading of the wrong card.)

Fix:
- ink_cuda_mem_info makes `dev` current for the call and restores the device.
  With it SQ-20 on 2 x 12 GiB is placed 1536 + 1536 experts, 0 in RAM.
- The peer clamp counts each layer's packed trunk bytes and the
  --vram-experts-gb cap (the trunk count is the reporter's hunk 1). The same
  clamp cuts back an explicit --layer-split and the even fallback; shed layers
  go to the stage before, so it works for 2 to 4 cards (the reporter's move at
  tier time only covered stage 1 -> 0).
- After the trunk is packed, each peer's real room is checked against its
  experts; a miss plans again with the measured loss as margin (max 3 tries).
- Guard (the reporter's hunk 3): a host expert on a peer stage fails the load
  with a message instead of serving garbage.

(cherry picked from commit 68e9e2a287e9a212196a1c55f75cd367fb711bfe)
```

### `e83de74d7d62` — fix(serve): /v1/responses sends a keep-alive while a custom tool call (Codex apply_patch) is written, so the stream is no longer silent for the whole call (#7132)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+9/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/test_responses_custom_keepalive_7132.py` (+95/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
The Qwen-form parser streams a custom tool's string parameter as tool_args events. The token loop counts any parser event as output, so its 10 s ping never
fired, and the Responses assembler drops those events for a custom call (the input goes out once, at its end). Nothing went out between
response.output_item.added and the end of the call; a client or proxy that cuts an idle stream (125 s on the reporter's Cloudflare path) ended a healthy
request. events() of _responses now remembers when a byte last left and yields a keep-alive when nothing has for KEEPALIVE_S.

Measured with a scripted engine (0.03 s a token, 20.3 s call, KEEPALIVE_S 1 s): longest gap 18.76 s before, 1.64 s after. The same call as a function tool
on the same route, on chat and on /v1/messages was 1.01 s. Inkling was not affected (its parser holds the arguments, so the ping fires).

New test serve/test_responses_custom_keepalive_7132.py (3): a custom call of 4.8 s keeps its longest gap under 2 s (4.36 s without the fix), the call is
unchanged (one input delta, completed), a function tool and a short answer are untouched. serve/test_responses.py: 73 passed.
```

### `327e9438114a` — fix(serve): a thinking budget a client asks for is capped at the default of its level on Inkling-2-Mini; the default client's 31,999 now thinks at most 8,192 tokens (#7133)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `app/host/server/run-config-check.mjs` (+2/-2)
  - `app/host/tests/run-config-check.test.mjs` (+8/-0)
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+1/-0)
  - `docs/MODELS.md` (+4/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+19/-2)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_client_budget_cap_7133.py` (+161/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
Since 0.2.21 the default request of the client in the issue (thinking enabled, budget_tokens 31999) gave Inkling level high AND a budget of 31,999 tokens with max_new 32,000, because
Service.reasoning_budget takes the request's number before the level's own default (high 8,192): minutes of hidden thinking a step at 25 to 60 tokens a second. v0.2.20
forced level 0 for Inkling, so this is a change of 0.2.21.

reasoning_budget now lowers a number that comes from the client (reasoning_budget_tokens, which the Messages route fills from budget_tokens, and the aliases
thinking_token_budget, thinking_budget, thinking_budget_tokens) to the default budget of the level the request runs at, and never raises one: 31,999 at high is 8,192, at
low 2,048; 1,024 at low stays 1,024. STRATA_INKLING_THINKING_BUDGET (one number for every level) is the cap when set, and 0 ("no default") lifts it. A request's own 0 still
means no budget; the run config's own reasoning_budget_tokens is not a client's and is not capped; other families are untouched. The first lowered request prints one line
that names the opt-out.

Opt-out: run config "inkling_budget_cap": false, or STRATA_INKLING_BUDGET_CAP=0 (also known to the app's run-config checker). Documented in docs/DETAILS.md and
docs/MODELS.md.

New tests serve/test_inkling_client_budget_cap_7133.py (15): the cap per level, smaller numbers kept, every alias, 0, both opt-outs, the one-number setting, the config's own
budget, other families, the once-only line, that default request through the frontend (level high, budget 31999 asked, 8192 allowed, side calls with 1,024 kept) and a
scripted engine that thinks 12,150 tokens (cut at 8,192 with the cap; thinks it all without). The Inkling serve tests (80) pass; app/host run-config-check test added (runs on
a box with node_modules).
```

### `7ab0d923c275` — fix(engine): the layer split's lend reservation takes half of its RAM on a fast drive, so decode gets its 9% back (#7089)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/expert_source.hpp` (+12/-0)
  - `engine/src/core/expert_source.cpp` (+82/-2)
  - `engine/tests/core/split_lend_complement_test.cpp` (+70/-0)
  - `terminal/data/settings-register.json` (+147/-114)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
From PR #7091 by @svonolfen (the STRATA_SPLIT_LEND_RESERVE switch, the bisect to 307ccd1ba and the measurements of #7089).

#6583 reserves RAM for the stages' prompt-path lend regions before the coldest tail of the expert complement is filled. The RAM budget is
fixed, so the reservation is paid out of the complement, the copy decode reads. On a RAM-tight 2-GPU layer split (reporter: RTX 4090 + 3090,
--resident-budget-gib 47 of 64 GiB, UD-Q4_K_XL, NVMe) the full reservation (2115 slots, 6.22 GiB) cost 9% decode (82.6 against 90.1 tok/s)
and bought no prefill (flat at 18K / 36K / 72K), because the borrowed slots read from a fast drive.

What it was for (#6583): 2 x RTX 3090 on Windows lost 9% prefill to 315 lend slots with no RAM copy, each re-read unbuffered every chunk.
There the drive is what makes the reservation pay, so it stays whole there.

Now STRATA_SPLIT_LEND_RESERVE decides how much of the lend regions' bytes the budget reserves first, counted from the highest slot of each
region (the slots a short prompt borrows):
  0 none (PR #7091: the complement first, as before #6583), full / 100 all, 1..99 that percent, auto or unset by the drive.
  auto: half when the drive's measured random 1 MiB O_DIRECT read rate is 3 GB/s or more (the probe the file tier's choice already uses,
  Linux), all when it is lower, and all where nothing measures it (Windows, macOS): the 0.1.67 behaviour stays wherever the drive is not
  known to be fast.
The reserved first part of every region is appended first, then what is left of the regions while the budget lasts, so the reserved
slots are certain to be kept and the two stages stay balanced. The startup line says the percent and why.

Answers do not change: the same experts are read from RAM or from the file, only which ones differ.

Tests: split_lend_complement_test learns the percent rule (unmeasured, slow, fast, the setting, bad values) and the fixture at full / 50 / 0.
Settings register entry for STRATA_SPLIT_LEND_RESERVE (rollback).
```

### `4fd373cfea1f` — build(release): every engine package keeps a symbol file (PDB, .debug, dSYM), outside the package and the release assets

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/CMakeLists.txt` (+2/-1)
  - `engine/cmake/strata_symbols.cmake` (+32/-0)
  - `engine/metal/CMakeLists.txt` (+1/-0)
  - `engine/spark/build_engine.sh` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/CMakeLists.txt` (+1/-0)
  - `engine/sycl/CMakeLists.txt` (+1/-0)
  - `terminal/tools/vision/CMakeLists.txt` (+1/-0)
  - `tools/packaging/UPDATES.md` (+20/-0)
  - `tools/packaging/build_cuda_linux.sh` (+5/-2)
  - `tools/packaging/build_cuda_linux_aarch64.sh` (+3/-0)
  - `tools/packaging/build_cuda_windows.py` (+17/-0)
  - `tools/packaging/build_hip_linux.sh` (+5/-3)
  - `tools/packaging/build_sycl_linux.sh` (+3/-1)
  - `tools/packaging/build_vision_windows.bat` (+1/-1)
  - `tools/packaging/keep_symbols.py` (+338/-0)
  - `tools/packaging/mac/build_engine.sh` (+3/-1)
  - `tools/packaging/symbols_build.sh` (+20/-0)
  - `tools/test_keep_symbols.py` (+68/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `cmake-upstream-build`: upstream — Берём version 0.1.70, symbols, sm120/draft-rule tests и dot-x4 policy; fork parity tests сохраняются отдельно.

**Original commit message**

```
Owner decision 2026-11-25: the debug symbols of every release build are kept from 0.2.22 on.

- engine/cmake/strata_symbols.cmake, option STRATA_KEEP_SYMBOLS (off by default; every release script turns it on): host debug information
  only, no flag that changes the machine code. MSVC: /Z7 and /DEBUG with /OPT:REF /OPT:ICF given again (/DEBUG alone turns the default
  folding off, which would change the code) and /PDBALTPATH so the exe does not name the build folder; nvcc: the host compiler's -g, never -G.
  clang / gcc: -g, with -Xarch_host for the HIP and SYCL device passes. Included by engine/, engine/src/inkling2 (its standalone build had no
  PDB at all), engine/sycl, engine/metal and terminal/tools/vision.
- tools/packaging/keep_symbols.py: `elf` (objcopy --only-keep-debug, --strip-debug on the stage copy, .gnu_debuglink; fails if .text or
  .nv_fatbin changed), `pe` (copies the PDB after checking its GUID against the exe's CodeView record), `macho` (dsymutil, strip -S),
  `check` (compares .text and the CUDA fatbin of a build with and without symbols), `show` (the build id). Every command writes symbols.json
  (id, sha256 of the shipped binary, sha256 of the symbol file). tools/test_keep_symbols.py.
- tools/packaging/symbols_build.sh (sourced by the Linux and Mac scripts), and the hooks in build_cuda_linux.sh, build_cuda_linux_aarch64.sh
  (+ engine/spark/build_engine.sh), build_sycl_linux.sh, build_hip_linux.sh, mac/build_engine.sh, build_cuda_windows.py and
  build_vision_windows.bat. A release build whose binary has no debug information stops (--require); STRATA_KEEP_SYMBOLS=0 builds without.
- tools/packaging/UPDATES.md, "Releasing": the Symbols step of the checklist (store C:\r0222\symbols\<package name>\, never uploaded).
```

### `09d8afb1abb5` — fix(serve): a request that names no sampling gets Inkling-2-Mini's model-card sampling (temperature 1.0), not the engine's greedy picking (#7092)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+5/-0)
  - `docs/MODELS.md` (+4/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/server_parts/http_api.py` (+5/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_card_sampling_7092.py` (+129/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The engine's own default is temperature 0.0, top_p 1.0, top_k 64 (ink_serve.h), i.e. greedy for a request with no sampling field, and Control had no model-card entry for
Inkling (MODEL_CARD_SAMPLING held qwen4exp, qwen35moe and hy4flash only). Greedy picking is the classic way to loop in an agent harness.

Source of the values: the model card's generation_config.json at huggingface.co/thinkingmachines/Inkling-2-Mini, read 2026-11-25 (read only):
  {"do_sample": true, "temperature": 1.0, "eos_token_id": 200006, "pad_token_id": 199999}
The README and the Unsloth GGUF page name no other sampling value (no top_p, top_k, min_p or penalty; the README only says "We recommend setting limits on output tokens and
agent turns"), so only temperature 1.0 is set, for thinking and non-thinking alike, for the arch names inkling2_loop, inkling2 and inkling2_loop_text; the engine keeps its
top_k 64 and top_p 1.0. Nothing was invented for the other fields.

Precedence is unchanged: the request, the Chat settings shared with apps, the run config's sampling block, then the card; temperature 0 asked for is still greedy. Other
families keep their cards.

docs/DETAILS.md and docs/MODELS.md say it. New tests serve/test_inkling_card_sampling_7092.py (8): the table for the three arch names, the other cards unchanged, over HTTP on
chat and Messages routes (card applied; request, shared settings and config block win; 0 stays greedy), and Service.run with the scripted Inkling engine; the inkling / sampling
/ card serve tests (282) pass.
```

### `2aed34d4e6ea` — fix(engine): the layer split's lend reservation stays whole by default; STRATA_SPLIT_LEND_RESERVE=0 / a percent is the opt-in (#7089, owner decision)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+1/-0)
  - `engine/include/strata/core/expert_source.hpp` (+6/-11)
  - `engine/src/core/expert_source.cpp` (+11/-32)
  - `engine/tests/core/split_lend_complement_test.cpp` (+11/-13)
  - `terminal/data/settings-register.json` (+389/-387)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
From PR #7091 by @svonolfen (the switch and the measurements).

The first version of this fix reserved half of the lend regions on a drive that reads 3 GB/s or more. Measured on 2 x RTX A6000 made RAM-tight
like the reporter's PC (9 interleaved rounds, same 2170-slot / 6.3 GiB lend regions): no decode gain at 50% (median ratio 0.996 and 1.012,
run-to-run spread about 5%), cold 18K-token prefill 5 to 7% slower in every round. At 0% decode +5.5% (4 of 4 rounds) and cold 18K prefill
-17%. The reporter's own table has the same shape (decode +9%, prefill at 18K -4.6%, at 36K -0.8%). A half-way default has a cost and no
measured gain, so the default is back to the whole reservation (0.1.66 / 0.1.67 behaviour) on every drive, and the drive probe is gone.

STRATA_SPLIT_LEND_RESERVE stays as an opt-in: 0 fills the expert complement first (the order before #6583, the reporter's PR), 1..99 reserves
that percent of the borrowed slots' bytes counted from the highest slot of each stage, unset / full / 100 / anything else reserves all. The
reserved first part of every region is appended first, so the reserved slots are certain to be kept and the stages stay balanced; with the
default nothing in the placement changes. The startup line names the percent when it is not 100.

Answers do not change in any setting (the same experts are read from RAM or from the file; gate on 2 x A6000: KL 0, identical ids, noise floor 0).

docs/DETAILS.md describes the setting and the measured trade; the settings register entry is rollback / off by default; the unit tests
cover the rule (unset / auto / full / 0 / percent / bad values) and the fixture at full / 50 / 0.
```

### `a5bfe0369bfb` — build(release): symbol flags that never change the machine code: no HIP-language flag, icpx -g1, AppleClang line tables, sha1 build id on SYCL

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/cmake/strata_symbols.cmake` (+13/-6)
  - `tools/packaging/build_sycl_linux.sh` (+1/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Measured by the packaging forks with a control built in the same directory (owner decision 2026-11-25, option a: the shipped binary is the symbol build, so it
must be the same code):
- HIP: -Xarch_host -g changed .text, .hip_fatbin and .rodata of the Linux HIP engine and the Windows clang one: removed in both branches of
  engine/cmake/strata_symbols.cmake (HIP-language files get no flag; the C++ files keep -g).
- icpx (SYCL): -Xarch_host -g1, line tables only, byte-equal.
- AppleClang (Metal): -gline-tables-only; tools/packaging/mac/build_engine.sh must show __text byte-equal against a build without it, else ship no DWARF there.
- nvcc host compiler: -Xcompiler -g stays, to be shown equal (.text and .nv_fatbin) by the same-directory control of each CUDA package.
- tools/packaging/build_sycl_linux.sh passes -Wl,--build-id=sha1 so the symbol file can be found by the build id.
```

### `88f4f39713ef` — fix(serve): only the coding client's DEFAULT thinking budget (budget_tokens 31999 on /v1/messages) is held to the default of its level on Inkling; every other budget goes through as sent (#7133)

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/DETAILS.md` (+1/-1)
  - `docs/MODELS.md` (+5/-4)
  - `terminal/serve/frontend.py` (+7/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+10/-7)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_client_budget_cap_7133.py` (+102/-49)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
Owner decision (option B) on top of the cap of the earlier #7133 commit, which lowered every number a client sent. Now:

- the Messages-API frontend (frontend.py) marks a request whose thinking.budget_tokens is exactly 31999 (CLIENT_DEFAULT_THINKING_BUDGET) and that carries no reasoning_budget_tokens of
  its own, with strata_default_thinking_budget.
- Service.reasoning_budget lowers a number to the default budget of the level the request runs at (31,999 at high is 8,192) only for a marked request. Any other budget goes
  through as before, still limited by the context and max_tokens: 20,000, 32,000, 31,998, a reasoning_budget_tokens or alias sent by a client (31,999 included), a budget with the
  OpenAI route's reasoning_effort and max_completion_tokens. A request's own 0 still means no budget; the run config's own budget and other families are untouched.
- Opt-out kept: run config "inkling_budget_cap": false, or STRATA_INKLING_BUDGET_CAP=0, lets the default use the full 31,999. The once-only log line now says "the client's default
  thinking budget of 31999 is held to 8192 ..." and is printed only for a held request.

Detection is by the number alone. The coding client's request headers (a beta-features list and a user-agent naming it) were not used: they were not observed in this work, a proxy
can remove or rewrite them, and the number is what makes the request slow, so a header would only narrow the rule (a 31,999 from another client would pass uncapped).

docs/DETAILS.md (inkling_budget_cap row) and docs/MODELS.md say it. serve/test_inkling_client_budget_cap_7133.py rewritten (22 tests): capped for the marked 31,999 at every level; uncapped for
20,000, 32,000, 31,998, 5,000, 100,000, every alias and a 31,999 not marked; the OpenAI reasoning_effort high with max_completion_tokens 32,000 (level default 8,192, no budget number) and
with an explicit reasoning_budget_tokens 20,000; both opt-outs; the one-number setting; the config's budget; other families; the once-only line; the default request through the
frontend; a scripted engine that thinks 12,150 tokens (cut at 8,192 for the marked default; whole for 20,000, for the opt-out; cut at 3,000 for a 3,000 budget).
```

### `d0717815c240` — build(release): the Windows clang (ROCm HIP) build takes -gline-tables-only for C and C++ files, so its code does not change

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/cmake/strata_symbols.cmake` (+5/-3)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
A same-directory control of the Windows HIP rdna3 strata.exe (jobq owner-20261125-1250-2a5a: the symbol build against a build of the same tree in the same folder
without STRATA_KEEP_SYMBOLS) still had a different .text (8,066,560 against 8,067,072 bytes) with -g on the C and C++ files. The compile of one file shows why:
expert_source.cpp assembled with -g has 10 of its 108,318 instruction lines different from the build without a flag (lea (%rdi,%rdx),%r8; add %r8,%rcx where the
other has two adds): the same arithmetic, another instruction selection. -gline-tables-only and -g1 give assembly identical to no flag on expert_source.cpp,
native_expert.cpp, session.cpp and remote_experts.cpp, and keep the file and line of every function, which is what a crash address needs (no variable or type
information). tools/packaging/keep_symbols.py check on the two strata.exe is the control the release script owner runs.
```

### `c234774038d1` — fix(packaging): bundle libgomp.so.1 with the engine, like libatomic.so.1

- Date: 2026-11-25
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `tools/docker/Dockerfile.src` (+1/-1)
  - `tools/packaging/collect_libs.py` (+4/-2)
  - `tools/packaging/notices/libgomp.txt` (+14/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
From PR #7189 by @rafal-prasal, applied unchanged.

The source Docker build (tools/docker/Dockerfile.src, BUILD_INKLING=1) stopped at
"strata-inkling needs libgomp.so.1": strata-inkling is linked against OpenMP,
binary_deps.py does not count libgomp as a base system library since 0.2.14, and
collect_libs.py left it to the OS.

- collect_libs.py: libgomp.so.1 joins BUNDLE_FROM_SYSTEM, so it is copied from the
  build machine with its notice, like libatomic.so.1.
- tools/packaging/notices/libgomp.txt: the notice (GPL-3.0-or-later WITH
  GCC-exception-3.1).
- Dockerfile.src: the inkling2 shape passes --include 'licenses/*', so the notice
  travels in the package.
```

### `0420a656e11e` — fix(artifact): read the 16 KiB-aligned .strata v1.1 layout (SQ-20 re-adjusted), keep the 4 KiB one exactly as before

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/artifact/strata_layout.hpp` (+249/-0)
  - `engine/include/strata/artifact/strata_reader.hpp` (+21/-0)
  - `engine/src/artifact/strata_reader.cpp` (+40/-36)
  - `engine/src/hy4flash/metal/strata_reader_nozstd.cpp` (+29/-26)
  - `engine/src/hy4flash/run/hy4_cpu_experts.cpp` (+16/-8)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/CMakeLists.txt` (+7/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_main.cpp` (+1/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_vision.h` (+2/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_common.h` (+12/-18)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_main.cpp` (+9/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_vision.h` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/sycl/ink_sy_common.h` (+22/-11)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/sycl/ink_sy_vision.h` (+6/-2)
  - `engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_golden.inc` (+89/-0)
  - `engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_test.cpp` (+561/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The re-adjusted Inkling-2-Mini-SQ-20 and Vision files have their section table at 0x20 (a u16 tile_layout_id
at 0x10), sections 16 KiB aligned and Section 3 padded to 16 KiB. Every reader of 0.2.22 reads the table at 0x10, so
it sees six empty sections and an empty manifest: StrataFileReader::open succeeds and the loader fails later with
"manifest parse error: JSON: unexpected char", with a Vision file refused as profile 2.

strata_layout.hpp (new, header only) is the one place that tells the layouts apart: both section tables are checked
(section_id equals the index, 4 KiB / 16 KiB alignment, inside the file, no overlap) and a header that is neither, or
both, is refused with a message that says why. A u64 below 4096 at 0x10 is not used for the decision. Section 3
of the 16 KiB layout takes its entry count from the manifest (top-level num_physical_layers x num_experts_per_layer,
found by walking the JSON, with each factor capped so the arithmetic cannot wrap), so a padded length that happens to be a
multiple of 34 is not miscounted, and a nested key or a chat template that spells the key is not taken for it.

Callers: StrataFileReader (CUDA, HIP, CPU Inkling, Hy4 CUDA/HIP/SYCL/CPU), the no-zstd Mac reader, the Metal and SYCL Inkling
loaders (header, Section 3 count), the Metal and SYCL vision loaders, the Hy4 CPU expert directory. The Inkling
Vision shard is recognised by header profile 3 or by arch inkling2_vision in the manifest (the new file has profile 2).

Test: engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_test.cpp (CMake: strata_reader_layout_test), with the real 0.2.22 headers of
SQ-20, SQ-10 and Vision as golden frames.
```

### `227d01b80a80` — fix(tools): the Python .strata readers take the 16 KiB-aligned layout too, old files read as before

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/sq20-new.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/sq20-old.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/vision-new.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/vision-old.hdr`
  - `terminal/tools/hy4_tokenizer.py` (+34/-1)
  - `terminal/tools/inkling_tokenizer.py` (+34/-1)
  - `terminal/tools/test_strata_header_layouts.py` (+257/-0)
  - `tools/squant/strata_format.py` (+40/-8)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
inkling_tokenizer.read_manifest, hy4_tokenizer.read_manifest and tools/squant/strata_format.parse_header_frame read
the section table at 0x10 only. On the re-adjusted Inkling SQ-20 and Vision (table at 0x20, a u16 tile_layout_id at
0x10) that is (offset 0, length 0): no manifest, so the model preview ("invalid .strata manifest"), adopt and the
server's tokenizer extraction failed on the new files.

The tokenizer readers find Section 0 through _section0(), the squant reader through detect_layout(), with the engine's
rule (strata_layout.hpp): the 16 KiB table is used only when it is valid (section_id equals the index, 16 KiB aligned,
inside the file, no overlap) and the 4 KiB one is not; a header valid as both is refused as ambiguous; anything else is
read as the 4 KiB layout exactly as before. build_header_frame can write the 16 KiB header (layout_16k=True, for tests);
verify_strata_file passes the file size.

Test: terminal/tools/test_strata_header_layouts.py, with the 448-byte header frames of the real old and new SQ-20 and
Vision in terminal/tests/data/strata_headers (14 tests; the 4 KiB ones pass on the old code too, the 16 KiB ones do not).
```

### `22f73e018a0c` — merge(inkling2-cuda): RTX 5060 Ti engine into the shared sm_120 base; one switch set, one card table, one hook, one MoE dispatch

**Original commit message**

```
Merges the engine part of the owner's handoff/gpu-rtx5060ti-1126 (44ee318f3, 1ddeb5b99; a959b2715) into the RTX 5090 import
(handoff/gpu-rtx5090-1126, 70b0b0a9e) and unifies the two. The reader parts of both branches are not taken (f-reader16k replaces them).

One switch set (strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp, no CUDA dependency, unit test ink_sm120_table_test):
  STRATA_INK_SM120=1 (fast), STRATA_INK_SM120_EXACT=1, STRATA_INK_SM120_<PART>=0|1, STRATA_INK_SM120_ROW=<id>;
  the old names STRATA_INK_RTX5090[_EXACT], STRATA_INK_RTX5060TI[_EXACT], STRATA_INK_5060TI_<x> are aliases (the new name wins).
  The default is ONE constant (kSm120FastDefault, false): the flip after the FASTQ lane is a one-line change.
  A switch on a card that is not sm_120 (cc 12.0) is refused with one stderr line; a cc 12.0 card without a measured row (only the 5090 and the
  16 GB 5060 Ti are measured) keeps the old path; the 5070 and 5050 rows are placeholders for the rentals.
  The card is matched by name, number of SMs and VRAM (the 5090 hand-over matched every cc 12.0 card, the 5060 Ti one no card at all).
One card table: per card the parts that run, how the FP8 trunk is held (cache beside the BF16 trunk / in place), which multi-user drafting
  implementation sits behind the hook, MoE tile sizes and kernel variant per batch range, chunk sizes, workspace reserves, drafting numbers.
One multi-user drafting hook in ink_backend.h (supports_batched_dspark / dspark_spec_step_batched_greedy / batched_dspark_prefill_first /
  max_macro_prefill_tokens / prefers_candidate_rows); the CUDA backend dispatches to the 5090 Mode-3 batched spec or the 5060 Ti K8V4 chain verify.
One MoE dispatch (ink_moe_fp4_sm120.cu): sm120::moe_*_tc take the card's variant per batch range; the two hand-overs' kernels are kept as variants
  (Base, Bk128, Direct, Tr128, Tr128Small, Tr128Bk128) until the A/B runs decide per card and range; STRATA_INK_SM120_MOE_FORCE forces one for A/B.
One VRAM sizing function pair at the top of ink_cuda_backend.cu (plan_vram_reserve / plan_prefill_chunk) for the long-context lane to change.

Review findings fixed here: the 5060 Ti workspace reserve (all-VRAM branch) that moved the K/V split 4352 -> 4096 on an A4000 now applies only with
the compact part on; kInkMaxTreeDepth is 8 again (deep trees use the 5090's 32 x 32 table, kInkDeepTreeDepth = 24, also for the PLD graft);
raw __dp4a replaced by dp4a_i8 (cuda12 sm_60 release build); sm_61 Q8_ROW INT8 predicate restored to the shipped one; the elastic KV/SSD tier is on
its own switch (STRATA_INK_ELASTIC_1M) and no longer part of the fast mode; the SSD file gets O_EXCL|O_NOFOLLOW in a private per-user directory, a
free-space check, loud failure on a write/read error, and a locked page_written map; the cold-store 16 KiB changes are opt-in only; the per-forward
token copy is gated; the --bench loop reads no environment; one cublasLt loader (CUDA 13 name first, an already-loaded library wins, no PATH
search on Windows) and ${CMAKE_DL_LIBS}.
```

### `4d766638e9f2` — fix(tools): the Python .strata readers take the 16 KiB-aligned layout too, old files read as before

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/sq20-new.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/sq20-old.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/vision-new.hdr`
  - `terminal/tests/data/strata_headers/vision-old.hdr`
  - `terminal/tools/hy4_tokenizer.py` (+34/-1)
  - `terminal/tools/inkling_tokenizer.py` (+34/-1)
  - `terminal/tools/test_strata_header_layouts.py` (+314/-0)
  - `tools/squant/strata_format.py` (+40/-8)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
inkling_tokenizer.read_manifest, hy4_tokenizer.read_manifest and tools/squant/strata_format.parse_header_frame read
the section table at 0x10 only. On the re-adjusted Inkling SQ-20 and Vision (table at 0x20, a u16 tile_layout_id at
0x10) that is (offset 0, length 0): no manifest, so the model preview ("invalid .strata manifest"), adopt and the
server's tokenizer extraction failed on the new files.

The tokenizer readers find Section 0 through _section0(), the squant reader through detect_layout(), with the engine's
rule (strata_layout.hpp): the 16 KiB table is used only when it is valid (section_id equals the index, 16 KiB aligned,
inside the file, no overlap) and the 4 KiB one is not; a header valid as both is refused as ambiguous; anything else is
read as the 4 KiB layout exactly as before. build_header_frame can write the 16 KiB header (layout_16k=True, for tests);
verify_strata_file passes the file size.

Test: terminal/tools/test_strata_header_layouts.py, with the 448-byte header frames of the real old and new SQ-20 and
Vision in terminal/tests/data/strata_headers (19 tests; the 4 KiB ones pass on the old code too, the 16 KiB ones do not; the header-location ones need no zstd module).
```

**Original commit message**

```
FASTQ on the 5060 Ti failed with COMPACT on (tool calls 6/26 vs 23/26, MATH 27/50 vs 46/50), the 5090 fast set passed. COMPACT is no longer
in the small-card fast set (it comes back after a FASTQ pass); STRATA_INK_SM120_COMPACT=1 still asks for it by name. COMPACT_XCODE and COMPACT_KV
switch the layer 20-23 transcode and the FP8 KV cache apart; both are on when COMPACT=1 and not named. Table test and docs updated.

Builds on the RTX 5090 and RTX 5060 Ti hand-over branches.
```

### `298de5d4eb2a` — feat(inkling2-cuda): one FP8 trunk for every RTX 50 card (the 5090 cache engine); the 5060 Ti in-place FP8 is explicit-only

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/INKLING_SM120.md` (+2/-1)
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+17/-12)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_sm120_cfg.cuh` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_sm120_table_test.cpp` (+4/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
FASTQ on the 5060 Ti failed with its in-place FP8 trunk (tool calls 6/26 against 23/26 off; 24/26 with FP8_TRUNK=0), the 5090 FP8 trunk passed every
suite. The table rows of the small cards now use the 5090 FP8 cache engine (it does not start when the card has no room for the copy, and the BF16 /
Q8_ROW GEMMs run); the 5060 Ti row reserves the 5090's 768 MiB for it in the K/V sizing as a placeholder. The in-place engine stays in the tree behind
STRATA_INK_SM120_FP8_INPLACE=1 only, in no fast set, until its defect is found.

Builds on the RTX 5090 and RTX 5060 Ti hand-over branches.
```

### `5e140c8d9802` — fix(inkling2-cuda): the 5060 Ti exact set loses PACK and PREFILL (they change tokens on a hybrid SQ-20 load)

  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+3/-1)

- Related decisions:

```
Bisect on a 5060 Ti with the new SQ-20 (off against exact minus parts, gen:30 and gen:9000): PACK moves experts between the CPU and the GPU lane and
PREFILL changes the prefill numerics; with both off, tokens are identical to off at 30 and 9000 prompt tokens.

Builds on the RTX 5090 and RTX 5060 Ti hand-over branches.
```

### `c609f6430270` — fix(inkling2-cuda): the 5060 Ti exact set is C64_SPEC + L2PF (PLD and CORE_GRAPH change tokens)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Measured on a 5060 Ti with the new SQ-20 (off against exact variants, 2 rounds, gen:30, gen:9000, gen:12000): PLD changes the tokens at gen:9000, CORE_GRAPH
at gen:30 from token 67 when combined with the other parts. C64_SPEC + L2PF: identical at all three, speed equal to off.

```

### `4fefe2e0770b` — fix(inkling2-cuda): COMPACT with COMPACT_KV=0 keeps the shipped workspace reserve

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+2/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The small all-VRAM reserve of compact assumed the FP8 KV cache. With COMPACT=1 COMPACT_KV=0 on a 5060 Ti (new SQ-20) it left 43 MiB free, the tree CUDA graph
capture failed with out of memory and the engine died at the first top-k (invalid argument, ink_cuda_backend.cu:994). Without the FP8 KV the shipped reserve applies.

Builds on the RTX 5090 and RTX 5060 Ti hand-over branches.
```

### `382dd7d551ea` — fix(artifact): read the 16 KiB-aligned .strata v1.1 layout (SQ-20 re-adjusted), keep the 4 KiB one exactly as before

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/artifact/strata_layout.hpp` (+249/-0)
  - `engine/include/strata/artifact/strata_reader.hpp` (+21/-0)
  - `engine/src/artifact/strata_reader.cpp` (+40/-36)
  - `engine/src/hy4flash/metal/strata_reader_nozstd.cpp` (+29/-26)
  - `engine/src/hy4flash/run/hy4_cpu_experts.cpp` (+16/-8)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/CMakeLists.txt` (+7/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_main.cpp` (+1/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_vision.h` (+2/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_common.h` (+12/-18)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_main.cpp` (+9/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_vision.h` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/sycl/ink_sy_common.h` (+22/-11)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/sycl/ink_sy_vision.h` (+6/-2)
  - `engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_golden.inc` (+89/-0)
  - `engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_test.cpp` (+561/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The re-adjusted Inkling-2-Mini-SQ-20 and Vision files have their section table at 0x20 (a u16 tile_layout_id
at 0x10), sections 16 KiB aligned and Section 3 padded to 16 KiB. Every reader of 0.2.22 reads the table at 0x10, so
it sees six empty sections and an empty manifest: StrataFileReader::open succeeds and the loader fails later with
"manifest parse error: JSON: unexpected char", with a Vision file refused as profile 2.

strata_layout.hpp (new, header only) is the one place that tells the layouts apart: both section tables are checked
(section_id equals the index, 4 KiB / 16 KiB alignment, inside the file, no overlap) and a header that is neither, or
both, is refused with a message that says why. A u64 below 4096 at 0x10 is not used for the decision. Section 3
of the 16 KiB layout takes its entry count from the manifest (top-level num_physical_layers x num_experts_per_layer,
found by walking the JSON, with each factor capped so the arithmetic cannot wrap), so a padded length that happens to be a
multiple of 34 is not miscounted, and a nested key or a chat template that spells the key is not taken for it.

Callers: StrataFileReader (CUDA, HIP, CPU Inkling, Hy4 CUDA/HIP/SYCL/CPU), the no-zstd Mac reader, the Metal and SYCL Inkling
loaders (header, Section 3 count), the Metal and SYCL vision loaders, the Hy4 CPU expert directory. The Inkling
Vision shard is recognised by header profile 3 or by arch inkling2_vision in the manifest (the new file has profile 2).

Test: engine/tests/quant/strata_reader_layout_test.cpp (CMake: strata_reader_layout_test), with the real 0.2.22 headers of
SQ-20, SQ-10 and Vision as golden frames.
```

  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_main.cpp` (+14/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_tail_kl.h` (+286/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The CUDA router can drop the rank 2..8 experts whose sigmoid share is at or below a threshold (optionally capped by the
cumulative dropped share, with or without renormalising the kept weights), per visit mask. A dropped slot gets expert id -1, weight 0
and a zeroed y_pair row; k_ink_moe_bin skips id -1, so the expert is neither read nor computed. Not set: the router runs as before.
Not applied on the hybrid host-streamed path or the dual-GPU expert-parallel path.

strata-inkling --tail-kl CORPUS --tail-cfgs CFGS --tail-out DIR runs the exact baseline and every configuration in one process over
fixed token sequences (one chunked prefill pass each, no generation) and writes per-row KL, delta NLL and top-1 agreement, plus the
skipped-slot counters per visit.
```

### `835a4618e0cb` — inkling cuda: long-context study tools (attention-mass probe, one-read verify kernel), both off by default

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
STRATA_INK_ATTN_PROBE=<file> (debug only): for every probed decode step of a global visit, recompute the 24 heads' softmax from the KV cache in FP32 and write
the mass captured by the top-k KV blocks (budgets 1K..8K cells, blocks of 64 / 128, oracle / Quest bound / Quest + forced blocks), the relative change of each
head's attention output under that selection, and the needle's mass inside the selection. Reads only; needs STRATA_INK_NO_GRAPH=1; output unchanged.

STRATA_INK_VERIFY_SHARED_KV=1|all: a speculative verify step (mode 2, global visits) reads each K/V cell once per group of rows (STRATA_INK_VERIFY_SHARED_ROWS,
default 8) instead of once per row. Bit-identical to k_ink_flash_gqa_splitk (tests in ink_shared_test.cu; a start-up check disables the switch if a build rounds
differently). Default off, the old kernel is untouched.

ink_cuda_main.cu: --tokens @file and --print-toks for the standalone runner.

(cherry picked from commit 1cb531beb2bd5170ca74ad9385e20aa0d6da1419)
```

### `9a0b4653db92` — perfstep 1: programmatic dependent launch for the decode graphs (STRATA_INK_PDL, off by default for now)

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_attn.cuh` (+3/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda.cuh` (+1/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_kernels.cuh` (+16/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_moe_cuda.cuh` (+7/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl.cuh` (+197/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl_dev.cuh` (+19/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl_kernels.cuh` (+103/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_rtx5090_fp8.cuh` (+31/-15)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_rtx5090_kernels.cuh` (+6/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+6/-5)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_topk.cuh` (+3/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
After a decode graph is captured, every kernel->kernel edge whose downstream kernel is registered is rewritten to a programmatic edge on the
"all blocks of the upstream kernel have begun" port (CUDA 12.3+ / 13.0 graph edge data, sm_90 and above). The downstream kernel's blocks start while
the upstream kernel's last blocks still run and wait at griddepcontrol.wait (INK_PDL_WAIT, the first statement of every registered kernel, before any early
return) until the upstream kernel has completed and its writes are visible. Results are those of the plain graph; only the launch ramp overlaps.

- ink_pdl_dev.cuh: INK_PDL_WAIT (compiled out below sm_90 / CUDA 12.3, a no-op when the kernel is launched the plain way).
- ink_pdl.cuh: registry, graph clone + edge rewrite, pdl::instantiate (drop-in for cudaGraphInstantiate; falls back to the plain graph on any API refusal).
- ink_pdl_kernels.cuh: the registered kernels (decode chain of the old path and the 5090 fast path, M = 1..16 variants).
- INK_PDL_WAIT added to 38 kernels; the 5090 M=1 FP8 GEMV stages its constant weight row before the wait (same loads, same order of accumulation).
- pdl::instantiate replaces cudaGraphInstantiate at the five decode capture sites in ink_runner.cuh.
- Attention kernels are not touched (PERF-LONGCTX lane): their edges stay ordinary.

Switch: STRATA_INK_PDL=1 (default off until identity and speed are in PERF-STEP RESULTS.md), STRATA_INK_PDL_PORT=1 for the kernel-trigger port.
Design credit: starting the next kernel while the previous finishes (programmatic dependent launch, weights staged before the wait) is the technique of
Basalt (MIT, github.com/jesdga95/basalt, include/basalt/kernels/pdl.hpp); this change is a graph-level rewrite written for Strata and shares no code with it.
```

### `dc80279754d5` — perfstep 2a: the M=1 gate+up expert kernel prefetches the expert's down matrix into L2 (STRATA_INK_M1_DNPF=1, off until measured)

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_rtx5090_kernels.cuh` (+29/-2)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The 72 gate+up CTAs of a routed expert each issue bulk L2 prefetches (cp.async.bulk.prefetch.L2, 16 KiB per instruction) for one slice of that expert's down
matrix (SF4 1.49 MB, MX8 2.74 MB), so the down kernel that follows reads it from L2. Nothing is read into registers, nothing is written, no result changes.
The kernel gets one more argument (pf_dn); STRATA_INK_M1_DNPF=1 turns it on.
```

### `6ad0c0000466` — inkling cuda: tensor-core split-K decode attention for the global visits (TC_ATTN, fast only, off by default)

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+5/-3)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_attn_tc.cuh` (+583/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_attn_tc_test.cu` (+261/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+21/-4)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_sm120_cfg.cuh` (+2/-0)
  - `notices/basalt-MIT.txt` (+26/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
New kernel k_ink_attn_tc (ink_attn_tc.cuh): one block per (KV head, key slice, group of up to 16 verify rows), K/V tiles read once for the whole group,
converted to FP16 in shared memory (FP8 E4M3 and FP16 KV, host-tail cells included), mma.m16n8k16 for QK and PV, probabilities x 2^15 with the V scale
folded in, FP32 denominator, the verify tail (the row's own ancestors) exact in FP32 with the sink. It changes rounding, so it is a fast-mode part
(STRATA_INK_SM120_TC_ATTN, bit 16), in no default set and in no exact set until the long-context A/B and the KL check are done.

Kernel test (ink_attn_tc_test.cu) against the old scalar kernel on synthetic KV: relative L2 4-8e-4 (FP8) / 2.9e-4 (FP16); on an RTX 5070 at 128K, M=16:
0.96 ms against 9.99 ms per visit (10x), 5-10x from M=5 up.

Design inspired by the long-context decode attention of Basalt (https://github.com/jesdga95/basalt, MIT, Copyright (c) 2026 Jesus Gulfo): tensor-core
split-K, 32-key tiles, FP16 probabilities scaled by 2^15, FP32 denominator. No code copied; the licence is kept in notices/basalt-MIT.txt.

Also brings in LCTX's one-read verify kernel and attention probe (f-lctx 1cb531beb, both off by default) as a cherry-pick.
```

### `938a678656c8` — inkling cuda: FP8-KV tile loader of the prefill attention (register prefetch, bit-identical), 1.8-5.7x faster per visit

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pf_fp8_test.cu` (+111/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_rtx5090_kernels.cuh` (+53/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
k_ink_flash_gqa_pf_sm120 loaded FP8 E4M3 K/V tiles with the generic loader: 4 bytes per lane, convert, store, synchronously, twice per tile, no overlap with the
scores. That is why a prefill with FP8 KV runs at 4.3k tok/s at 128K while the FP16 KV (cp.async) runs at 11.8k (BASALT-GAP, RTX 5090).
New: 16-byte loads of the next K tile and the current V tile into registers at the top of the tile loop, converted after the softmax with the same arithmetic
(e4m3 -> f32, x per-cell scale, round to FP16), so the result is bit-identical to the generic loader. Global and SWA visits, KV in VRAM (host tail keeps the
generic loader). ink_pf_fp8_test.cu builds twice (-DSTRATA_PF_FP8_LEGACY = the generic loader) and prints a hash of the output: equal in all 6 cases.

RTX 5070, one visit, ms legacy -> new: global p0=0 M=1024 0.54 -> 0.30; p0=3000 M=2048 5.2 -> 2.1; p0=30000 M=4096 118 -> 21; swa M=1024 0.80 -> 0.51.
```

**Original commit message**

```
- ink_attn_tc.cuh: slices per KV head = two full waves of resident blocks (85 on a 5090, 24 on a 5070; STRATA_INK_TC_ATTN_SPLITS overrides), partial buffers sized
  for 96 + the tail; the runner takes the kernel for mode 0 rows up to 16 as well (the teacher-forced KL harness runs small chunks through it).
- ink_cuda_backend.cu plan_vram_reserve(): STRATA_INK_KV_FIRST=1 sets the K/V of the whole context, the DSpark state of every context token and a growth margin aside
  before the experts get the rest, on cards of up to 20 GiB and one slot, with a floor of 1.5 GiB for the experts (the placement rule of f-lctx, RESULTS-LCTX.md
  section 3: 64K 9.9 -> 28.5 tok/s, 128K 5.6 -> 23.0 on a 5060 Ti 16 GB). Off by default, not yet measured on the merged code.
- docs/INKLING_SM120.md: the TC_ATTN part.
- ink_attn_tc_test.cu: plain rows, bandwidth probe.
```

### `c649756156dc` — perfstep 1b: PDL does not start a kernel early behind a one-block kernel; per-kernel bisect switches

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl.cuh` (+30/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl_kernels.cuh` (+86/-82)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Measured on an RTX 5090 (plain decode, no drafter, 4-5 rounds, tokens identical in every round): PDL on every registered kernel made the exact path 11% slower
(137.6 -> 121.9 tok/s) and the fast path 5% faster (170.3 -> 179.3). The loss came from kernels started early behind the one-block kernels of the step (top-k,
MoE bin, the M=1 glue): their many resident blocks only spin. Edges whose upstream kernel has fewer than 2 blocks now keep their ordinary edge
(STRATA_INK_PDL_MINBLK, default 2): exact 139.1 tok/s (+1.1% over off), fast 178.8 (+5.0%).

Bisect tools: STRATA_INK_PDL_SKIP=a,b and STRATA_INK_PDL_ONLY=a,b (kernel-name substrings), STRATA_INK_PDL_MAXBLK=n (downstream block limit).
```

  - `mcp-upstream-features`: upstream — Берём stale-ok hardware refresh, custom models, Inkling legacy/update metadata; наша own-size строка сохраняется.

**Original commit message**

```
setup.py: inkling_fetch_dflash2 downloads dflash2.safetensors next to the model after the model itself, checks it against its pins (size, SHA-256), shows its size before the
download and counts it in the free-space check. A file that is there and checks out is kept; the persons's own folder (--gguf-dir) is never written to; a download that fails
or does not check out leaves no file behind and the engine uses DSpark (setup and update try again). update_install fetches it for every installed Inkling .strata model whose
folder is the models folder, so existing SQ-20 users get it on update. GGUF builds and other families are not touched. Tests: terminal/tests/terminal/test_inkling_dflash2_fetch.py.

Credit: tjm8874 / Basalt pull request 763 (MIT) for the DFlash 2 support in Basalt that the engine side follows; weights incoai/Inkling-2-Mini-DFlash2 (Apache-2.0).
```

### `73acfd256b9e` — inkling2 CUDA: native expert dot kernels for IQ2_XXS, IQ2_XS, IQ2_S, IQ3_S, IQ1_S and Q2_0

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_gguf_types_test.cu` (+250/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_iq_grids.cuh` (+1151/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_moe_cuda.cuh` (+165/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The GSQ-RCO IQ3_S file of Inkling 2 Mini picks a quant type per tensor and per layer. Six of its types had no GPU kernel, so the CUDA
loader requantised those 18 layers to SF4 (lossy, 4.49 MB per expert, about 13 GB of experts instead of 9.5 GB).

ink_gguf_row_dot_multi now reads them natively (DP4A on the Q8_1_G32 activations, same lane layout as the IQ3_XXS and Q4_K kernels),
ink_gguf_has_gpu_kernel and ink_gguf_row_bytes list them. The codebooks come from a generated header (ink_iq_grids.cuh, written by a
script from strata-quant-common.h). ink_gguf_types_test.cu compares each type's row dots (1 and 4 tokens) with a reference dequantisation
written from the block specs: worst relative error 1.5e-8 on an A4000 (sm_86); the file also builds for sm_120.
```

### `a5aa58b1ba2d` — perfstep 1c: PDL is on by default on the RTX 5090 row; the 5090 row's spec_cost goes from 12 to 8

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+1/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_pdl.cuh` (+17/-7)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
PDL: default on for compute capability 12.x cards with 160..192 SMs (the 5090, the only card measured); STRATA_INK_PDL=1 forces it on any sm_90+ card, =0 off.
Measured on the 5090, plain decode, 3 rounds, tokens identical: fast 171.3 -> 179.5 tok/s (+4.8 %), exact 138 -> 139.6 (+1.2 %).

spec_cost 12 -> 8 (the 5090 row; ink_sm120_table.hpp): the tree-size model of the DSpark drafter prices an extra verify row too high for this card
(a 12-row verify step is 2x a plain step, the model said 7.5x). C1 tok/s, fast, spec_cost 12 -> 8 (3 rounds): chat 315 -> 319, code 402 -> 458, count 404 -> 628,
json 266 -> 268, prose 222 -> 227, tool 414 -> 404. Exact mode (the row applies to it too): tokens identical to off (9/9 at 30, 3000 and 9000 tokens).
```

### `4c90917e5139` — inkling2 CUDA: compact trunk for GGUF files with GSQ-RCO expert types (one 12 GB card)

- Date: 2026-11-26
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+21/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_gguf_loader.h` (+112/-4)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_main.cpp` (+2/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_manifest.h` (+3/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
A GGUF reaches the CUDA runner with a BF16 trunk: embedding 0.93 GB, unembed 0.93 GB plus a screening index, attention 1.3 GB.
With the 9.5 GB of native experts of the GSQ-RCO IQ3_S file that does not fit a 12 GB card.

For a single-GPU sm_80+ run of a file whose experts use the RCO types (IQ1_S, IQ2_XXS, IQ2_XS, IQ2_S, IQ3_S gate/up, Q2_0 down), or with
STRATA_INK_GGUF_COMPACT=1 (=0 turns it off), the loader now
  - requantises the dense attention matrices (K-quants, IQ4_XS, BF16) to Q8_0 in the fragment order of the int8 GEMM (as it already did for
    stored Q8_0 matrices),
  - builds the unembed as FMT_Q8ROW (the layout the .strata trunk uses),
  - puts the token embedding last in Section 1 (manifest flag embed_host_tail).
The runner keeps that embedding in pinned mapped host RAM, uploads the rest, and shares the Q8_ROW unembed as the argmax screen.
Other GGUF files and the .strata path are unchanged.
```
