### `017b6b86519e` — engine(cuda): the Turing (sm_75) kernel series is on by default; STRATA_SM75_PERF=0 turns it off (#7041, #7076)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/CMakeLists.txt` (+6/-1)
  - `engine/include/strata/core/sm75_series.hpp` (+11/-9)
  - `engine/src/core/sm75_series_test.cpp` (+26/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - `cmake-upstream-build`: upstream — Берём version 0.1.70, symbols, sm120/draft-rule tests и dot-x4 policy; fork parity tests сохраняются отдельно.

**Original commit message**

```
Until 0.2.22 the bitwise Turing kernels (PRs #2069-#2072, #2081, #2091-#2093, #2145-#2146, #2220-#2223, #1821) were on only with STRATA_SM75_PERF=1, because
nobody on the team had an sm_75 card.  #7041 measured them on 2x RTX 2080 Ti: +4.7% to +21% cold prefill from 32K to 207K tokens, decode level.

Our own check, a rented RTX 2080 Ti, Qwen3-30B-A3B UD-Q4_K_XL, one binary, STRATA_SM75_PERF unset against =1, 7 interleaved pairs:
- the same ids on 12 prompts at temperature 0 (10 short, 16K, 32K), and in every pair (decode short and 4K, prefill 16K and 32K);
- cold prefill 16K / 32K level (B/A 0.999 / 0.999), 4K level (1.000); decode after a 4K prompt B/A 1.042; short decode B/A 0.967 with a spread of 49.4 to
  57.2 tok/s in the A arm alone, so within run-to-run noise (2 of 7 pairs faster);
- no warning or error line only in one arm.
The reporter's prefill gain did not show on that model (a different pack and card count), so this is "no change measured", not a confirmed speed-up.

STRATA_SM75_PERF unset or non-zero: on for a compute capability 7.5 card; =0: off (the 0.2.22 default).  Only 7.5 cards are ever affected (is_sm75; under
STRATA_EMULATE_CC=75 an sm_86 card runs the series for tests); each kernel keeps its own switch, which wins in both directions; the numerics-changing Turing
options (SELECT_SIMT, PA_V2T, PF_FUSED_SM75, BF16_TC) stay opt-in.  sm75_series_test covers the rule.
```

### `72f18fa6400f` — fix(inkling2-cuda): the hybrid plan holds back the whole DFlash 2 drafter below sm_80 (--parallel 4 ran a 2080 Ti out of memory)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+1/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
On a card below sm_80 the drafter's memory is larger than the plan counted: the BF16 matrices sit as FP32 (+112 MiB), the Q8 / Q8 + SF4 block weights, the scratch, and 42 MiB of ring per slot.
For one slot the 1850 MiB figure of the hybrid path covers it; with 4 slots the planned K/V + workspace side is the larger one and counted only the 42 MiB rings of DFlash 2 (168 MiB),
so after the load 2175 MiB were free, the runner + K/V took 1800 and DFlash 2 took 370 and the 5 MiB left died at the first request
("CUDA error 2 ... allocating 4 MiB; 1 of 11005 MiB free", RTX 2080 Ti 11 GB, SQ-20 v2, --parallel 4, 4 requests). df2_f32_path() cards now count the drafter as the RTX 50 branch does
(df2_total_mib minus 100 MiB that DSpark's pad already holds): 2539 MiB free after the load, 55 MiB left at the end of the run, and the run completes (C4 e2e 14.9 vs 15.8 tok/s for the
shipped DSpark build, 2 rounds). Cards of sm_80 and newer keep their sizing.
```

### `15cae0067f23` — sm120: no drafter for 2..15 slots on a card below 24 GiB; DFlash 2's rings stop while 128 MiB stay free; the COMPACT keep stays 128 MiB (crash proof)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_dflash2.cuh` (+3/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_dflash2_path.cuh` (+8/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_dspark.cuh` (+1/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+2/-2)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
No K/V keep change: the 128 MiB keep passed the crash proof on an RTX 5060 Ti (default settings, DFlash 2 mix beside the model, --parallel 1 --max-context 40000, FASTQ harness: GSM-100 + MATH-50 (149 of 150 right)
+ one 32K needle (pass); 0 fatal engine lines). The start ends with 161 MiB free; nvidia-smi used 15716 MiB after the load and 15798 MiB at the peak, so the lazy growth is 82 MiB and the lowest free is 161 - 82 = 79 MiB
(threshold 48). A 256 MiB keep would cut the device K/V from 7808 to about 1400 tokens (8K decode 71 -> about 30, prefill 3864 -> about 1556), so it is not taken.

2..15 slots (a card below 24 GiB, no STRATA_INK_DRAFTER): no drafter. There is no multi-user chain at those counts (COMPACT refuses them, every RTX 50 part is off) and DFlash 2's memory only costs experts: RTX 5060 Ti fast,
4 / 8 slots 43.5 / 53.0 tok/s with DFlash 2 against 50.6 / 63.3 without; exact 44.6 / 50.3 against 47.5 / 55.2. A pack that carries DSpark but runs without a drafter no longer keeps its reserve or builds the unembed screen
(has_dspark means "a drafter runs": the pack's DSpark only with STRATA_INK_DRAFTER=dspark, or DFlash 2's sidecar). Result: the default equals --draft off, 54.4 / 65.6 tok/s fast and 50.7 / 57.8 exact at 4 / 8 slots, with the same
resident experts (2093 / 1768 fast, 2003 / 1581 exact); the residency-first rule's second build_screen line (plan_for) got the same condition, without which the merged tree still built the 442 MiB screen (4 / 8 slots: 45.2 / 53.6 tok/s, 1976 / 1651 experts). STRATA_INK_DRAFTER=dflash2 still asks for the drafter by name.

16 and more slots, compact: the previous COMPACT commit added DFlash 2's real total to the headroom at every slot count, which at 16 slots (726 MiB) made COMPACT refuse and the engine fail to start ("not enough free GPU
memory for the working buffers", also with --draft off, which the loader cannot see). The real total is now added for 1 slot only, and the start-up rings stop while 128 MiB would stay free (before: all 8 rings, the start ended with 27 MiB
free). RTX 5060 Ti fast, DFlash 2 / no drafter e2e tok/s: 16 slots 212 / 191 (+11 percent, 8 rings, 183 MiB free at the end of the start), 32 slots 303 / 236 (+29, 7 rings, 163), 64 slots 350 / 238 (+47, 5 rings, 127).
```

**Original commit message**

```
Ornith-1.5-35B-A3B has the architecture, tensor layout, tokenizer and MTP
block of Qwen3.6-35B-A3B (qwen35moe), so it runs on the same engine path.
It is now a family of its own in the shared table: the four plain
quantizations the publisher's GGUF repository has (Q4_K_M, Q5_K_M, Q6_K,
Q8_0), pinned to one revision with sizes and SHA-256 values (checked against
Hugging Face), the picture reader, the MIT licence line, KV and fit figures
(q36's KV and state, a base figure with the dense part swapped; the staging
figures are q36's and marked as not measured for Ornith).

- `strata models import PATH --plan` reads an Ornith-named file as family
  ornith with its own size; any other name stays qwen36.
- Model ids "ornith-1.5-..." are Strata ids in `strata serve` and the app's
  gateway; the picture step leaves Ornith to setup like Qwen3.6; the launch
  display name is the family's.
- App: Ornith is its own display group after Qwen3.6 (its title contains
  "35B-A3B", which put it under Qwen3.6), with no KV choice like Qwen3.6, and
  the short name "Ornith Q4_K_M".
- Docs: a section in docs/MODELS.md and the README.
- Tests: terminal/tests/terminal/test_ornith_types.py with four fixtures (the
  tensor type tables read from the GGUF headers at the pinned revision),
  test_ornith_catalog_7134.py (model list, run config, pictures, model ids),
  app/host/tests/ornith-family.test.mjs.

Not measured by us: a load and answer of an Ornith file on our own box.

Patch and tests by eSPiYa (PR #7140)
```

### `c8613a0561c3` — fix(inkling2-cuda): dp4a_i8 uses the hardware dp4a on sm_70 (a V100 ran an emulation); the V100 head screen of DFlash 2 is the dp4a kernel

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda.cuh` (+3/-3)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_dflash2.cuh` (+1/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
dp4a_i8 took the hardware __dp4a only on sm_61 and sm_75 and newer, on the comment that PTX dp4a is emulated on sm_70. It is not: cuobjdump of a sm_70 module shows IDP.4A.S8.S8, and a probe
(phaseA-tests/rel0223/nv-older/dp4a_probe.cu, Tesla V100 SXM2, CUDA 12.8) runs 2667 G dp4a/s for __dp4a against 1265 G/s for the four-multiply form (2.1x). Only sm_60 (P100) has no dp4a.
The guard is now `__CUDA_ARCH__ >= 610`; sm_60 keeps the exact integer emulation. Integer arithmetic: the results are bit-identical.

V100 (SQ-20 v2 and the GSQ-RCO IQ3_S file, same sources, only the guard differs, 2 rounds, alternating):
  IQ3_S plain: prefill 4K 197 -> 207 tok/s, 600-token prompt 206 -> 220, 30-token 210 -> 252; decode 70.0 -> 74.5 / 68.6 -> 73.0 / 70.8 -> 75.7; ids identical 2/2 on all steps
  SQ-20 plain (--draft off): prefill 4K 2142 -> 2143, decode 60.5 -> 60.8 / 63.6 -> 63.8: no change, ids identical 2/2 (the SQ-20 kernels do not go through dp4a_i8)
  DFlash 2 (Q8 GEMVs and the screen): draft step 4.78 -> 3.43 ms (hardware dp4a, cuBLAS INT8 screen) -> 3.02 ms (with the dp4a screen kernel); decode prose 66.9 -> 69.7 -> 70.7, json 128.8 -> 132.4 -> 133.8,
  tool 87.5 -> 90.7 -> 91.7, 4K document 60.8 -> 63.2 -> 64.1 tok/s; tokens identical to the emulated build on all four prompts (2/2).
Because the dp4a screen kernel is faster than the cuBLAS INT8 GEMM on the V100, DFlash 2 uses the kernel there (sm_60 and sm_70); sm_61 and sm_75 keep the cuBLAS GEMM
(STRATA_INK_DFLASH2_SCREEN=cublas|kernel still picks either for an A/B).
```

### `d713d175faa9` — [Control] Ornith fit figures per size, from a real load of all four sizes (#7134)

- Date: 2026-11-27

```
The Ornith catalog entry took one base figure (q36's base with the dense
part swapped to 1.85 GiB, from the reporter's Q5_K_M log) for every size.
A real start of each size with `strata launch --family ornith` (the 0.2.23
Control tree, engine 0.1.69 CUDA 13 package, RTX 3090 24 GB, the files of
the pinned revision, --max-context 32768, 2026-11-27) printed this for the
weights that stay on the card: Q4_K_M 1.65 GiB, Q5_K_M 1.85 GiB, Q6_K
2.06 GiB, Q8_0 4.40 GiB. For Q8_0 the context advice was about 2.5 GiB too
optimistic. The base is now q36's base with each size's own dense figure
(DENSE_GPU_GIB_ORNITH). The staging figures stay q36's: the same starts
printed 0.00 (Q4_K_M), 1.10, 1.23 and 1.59 GiB, all at or below them.

All four sizes loaded, answered correctly (capital of Norway, a Fibonacci
function, a time sum) and accepted MTP drafts (about 70 to 85 percent);
decode on the 3090 was 150 to 207 tok/s (Q4_K_M), 119 to 149 (Q5_K_M),
88 to 118 (Q6_K) and 55 to 65 tok/s (Q8_0). The KV figure matched the
engine (1.43 GiB per session at 32768 tokens).

Test: the per-size figures are pinned in test_ornith_catalog_7134.py.
docs/MODELS.md says what was measured.
```

### `6f9b779ef910` — sm120 table: the GB10 row is measured; drafting numbers cost 5 / plain 0, an empty exact set

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+11/-6)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_sm120_table_test.cpp` (+14/-12)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Measured on a DGX Spark GB10 (Vast, SQ-20 v2 and IQ3_S, DFlash 2 as the drafter). The 5090's drafting numbers (spec_cost 8, plain margin 1.02) made
fast mode decode at the plain rate (53 tok/s) on prose and chat, below off (55 / 69). With spec_cost 5 and plain 0 the C1 decode is prose 79,
chat 94, json 99, code 115, count 183, tool 112 against off 55 / 69 / 81 / 78 / 97 / 81 (mean 113.6 against 76.8), prefill 2.5-2.8x. Dropping
MOE_OPT or the FP8 trunk lost speed, TC_ATTN lost at C1, PDL and SPEC_ADAPT changed nothing, so the fast set stays the 5090's big set.
The exact set is empty (kSm120SparkExact): DSPARK_OPT replaces the window numbers and the verify tree, and exact tokens then differed from off with a
drafter on (json, tool); without it exact = off on every prompt, with and without a drafter. default_on follows from the FASTQ subset.
Table test updated (measured count 3, the Spark cases).
```

### `7cebe7a6cf20` — feat(serve,launch): the engine's "DFlash 2 not loaded" line reaches the person, and --draft on forces the drafter through

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `docs/MODELS.md` (+3/-0)
  - `terminal/serve/inkling_kind.py` (+20/-5)
  - `terminal/serve/server.py` (+5/-1)
  - `terminal/serve/test_inkling_draft_veto_line.py` (+130/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_cli/launch/cmd.py` (+5/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_cli/launch/runner.py` (+4/-1)
  - `terminal/strata_cli/launch/settings.py` (+3/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - `qwen36-200k-launch`: ours — Upstream не заменяет fork launch profiles и static checker.

**Original commit message**

```
The Inkling engine (HIP now, CUDA through the residency-first drafter rule) leaves DFlash 2 out when loading it would move a model that fits whole into the
slower hybrid mode (or cut a noticeable part of the experts' room). It prints ONE line to its log, "DFlash 2 not loaded: it would move part of the model off
the card (...)", and says `--draft on` (or STRATA_INK_DFLASH2_FORCE=1) loads it anyway.

- Showing it: after READY the server reads this start's engine log (inkling_kind.veto_line); the line is printed as "[strata] DFlash 2 not loaded: ..." (what the
  Terminal's start and the app's start log read) and becomes the drafting note of /metrics and the Monitor, in place of the generic "see its log for why".
  `--draft off` is the person's own choice: nothing is repeated at them. `--draft on` counts as a drafter that was asked for.
- Forcing it: the word `on` of `--set draft=on` and of the Advanced screen used to read as "present", so the engine got a bare `--draft`; --draft is now a word-valued
  flag (settings.WORD_VALUE_FLAGS) and `on` / `off` go through as typed. `strata launch --draft on|off` is an explicit option (`--draft` alone was ambiguous
  between --drafting and --draft-vocab); the other models ignore it. The Inkling engine_args keep a `--draft on` already in the arguments.

Tests: serve/test_inkling_draft_veto_line.py, with a stub engine that prints the real line to stderr unless it is started with `--draft on` (47 passed with
the Inkling dialect and effort-tail tests, boston). docs/MODELS.md.
```

### `04c94cbe3246` — feat(inkling2-cuda): below sm_80 a hybrid load never gets the default drafter (principle 1b of the drafter rule), for .strata and .gguf

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_draft_rule.hpp` (+5/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+16/-3)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_draft_rule_test.cpp` (+14/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The residency-first rule (ink_draft_rule.hpp) vetoes the default DFlash 2 only when it would move a fully resident model to a hybrid one or cut the tier by 10 % or more. On the cards below
sm_80 (V100, RTX 20, GTX 10, P100) that left hybrid cases with a drafter that loses decode speed. SQ-20 v2, decode tok/s on prose, plain against DFlash 2: RTX 2080 Ti 11 GB 23.5 / 16.3,
P100 16 GB 25.1 / 22.1, GTX 1080 Ti 11 GB 12.7 / 9.2; the GSQ-RCO IQ3_S file on the 2080 Ti (DFlash 2 was loaded, the veto did not fire): prose 32.4 / 23.1, chat 35.9 / 29.1, code 36.0 / 30.3.
Every draft window reads more host experts than a plain step.

- InkDraftLoadIn::below_sm80 and principle 1b: below_sm80 && !full_without (some experts live in host memory, with or without the drafter) -> not loaded, the same veto line, `--draft on`
  (or STRATA_INK_DRAFTER=dflash2, STRATA_INK_DFLASH2_FORCE=1) loads it anyway, `--draft off` wins. A fully resident card below sm_80 (a V100 32 GB) keeps drafting.
- The backend applies the rule to a .gguf on those cards too (it was .strata only): the expert bytes are the file size minus the dense trunk (1.4 GiB compact GGUF trunk, 3.4 GiB otherwise).
- Unit test: the cases of the new principle (hybrid, the same load above sm_80, --draft on, --draft off, fully resident); `ink_draft_rule_test: ok` (g++ -std=c++17).

Proof on the Tesla P100 16 GB (sm_60, one binary built from this commit, 1 start per arm, default / `--draft on` / `--draft off`):
  SQ-20 v2 (hybrid, 2313 experts in VRAM / 759 host): default prints "[InkCUDA] DFlash 2 not loaded: it would move part of the model off the card (the experts need 14.6 GiB, 9.8 GiB fit with the
  drafter's 728 MiB, 10.5 GiB without it) ... `--draft on` loads it anyway." and runs with no drafter (decode 23.2 tok/s, ids identical to --draft off); `--draft on` loads DFlash 2 ("drafter on",
  2154 / 918 experts, decode 17.4); `--draft off` 22.7.
  IQ3_S (GSQ-RCO; all 3072 experts resident on the P100, so no hybrid load): default and `--draft on` load DFlash 2, `--draft off` does not, ids identical in all three: the rule does not
  fire on a fully resident card, as intended.
```

### `f78e234a2c5b` — engine: the drafter rule's context threshold: 2048 tokens on hybrid cards, 3072 on resident sm < 80 cards

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_draft_rule.hpp` (+15/-6)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+3/-1)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_draft_rule_test.cpp` (+9/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
kDraftPlainCtxHybrid = 2048 (was the provisional 3072), from RTX 5070 12 GB (SQ-20 v2, 3 rounds, DFlash 2 against --draft off, plain = no drafting):
- prompts of 33-62 tokens: compact hybrid load: prose 76.2 (DFlash 2) vs 86.0 (plain), code 86.3 vs 80.7, tool 98.8 vs 84.9; old path: 47.0 vs 62.6, 52.0 vs 57.7, 57.4 vs 63.3
- a 4096-token document: hybrid 34.5 vs 59.2 tok/s (-42%), old path 22.1 vs 44.1; acceptance 1.9 tokens per step
- 32K: level (30.5 / 30.5 hybrid, 26.4 / 26.7 old path)
NV-OLDER (V100 / P100 / 2080 Ti): DFlash 2 is also below plain on a 4K document (V100 -7% at 4K, -10% at 8K with all experts resident; its draft step is emulated), so a fully resident card
below sm_80 goes plain from kDraftPlainCtxSlowArch = 3072 tokens. Resident sm_80 and newer: unchanged (the 5090 and the PRO cards win at 4K with the window governor).
ink_draft_plain_step takes the compute capability (default 90, no effect for callers that do not pass it); the CUDA hook passes the device's. Unit test covers the measured cases.
```

### `50d9661d27e2` — Drafter rule: a plain step skips the whole drafting block; COMPACT_HYBRID is no longer refused by the all-VRAM end-of-start estimate

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+4/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+3/-2)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_backend.h` (+4/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_serve.h` (+1/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
1. The drafter rule's context cut (hybrid 2048, slow arch 3072) only made the fused tree step return 0; the serve loop then fell through to the
   dspark_draft + forward_batch path, which drafts as well, so the cut never took effect (RTX 5070 12 GB, 4K document: 27.8 tok/s, plain 58.6).
   New InkBackend::draft_plain_now(ctx_pos) (default false; CUDA: the rule with the hybrid flag, the --draft on override and the device cc);
   the single-stream drafting block in ink_serve.h skips on it. RTX 5070, SQ-20 v2, decode tok/s, 2 rounds, default path: 4K document 57.1
   (plain 58.6), 32K 30.3 (plain 30.4), code 84.7 (plain 80.3), tool 97.8 (plain 84.8), prose 72.0 (plain 85.8).
2. COMPACT_HYBRID has no all-VRAM end state, but the end-of-start estimate (runner + DFlash 2 vs free VRAM) still ran for it and read "0 MiB free" on
   every 8 / 12 GB card with the drafter, so the compact trunk was refused and the old path ran. The estimate now skips the hybrid case (the hybrid
   loader keeps its own reserve). RTX 5060 8 GB: C1 decode 14.8 vs 10.7 tok/s old path.
Open: the batched (2 or more slots) drafting path has no cut; the per-request governor (patch kept beside the results) was measured and not shipped:
it loses 15 to 20 % to plain on prose and the 4K document in 256-token runs.
```

### `12982f239deb` — sm120 table: the measured Ada / Ampere part sets (no PACK), the RTX 4060 Ti 16 GB and RTX 3060 12 GB rows measured but not default, the big-card guard

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+10/-4)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_sm120_table_test.cpp` (+12/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Measured on rented cards (SQ-20 v2, DFlash 2 on; RTX 3060 12 GB, RTX 4060 Ti 8 GB and 16 GB, RTX 3090 24 GB; rel0223/nv-ada-ampere/RESULTS.md):

- The small-card set (COMPACT, COMPACT_HYBRID on 12 GB and less, PLD, CORE_GRAPH, C64_SPEC, TC_ATTN) loses PACK: no gain with every expert resident and
  -30% at 9K on a hybrid load. PF_ATTN stays out (+14-18% prefill at 9-12K, but -16% at 16K and -39% at 32K on the 16 GB compact card).
- Rows "4060ti-16" and "3060" are measured (STRATA_INK_SM120=1 uses them without STRATA_INK_SM120_ROW) and default_on = false: KL against the old path is
  0.0055-0.0107 on four corpora (floor 0.0287), the FASTQ subset passes except the agent suite on the 16 GB card (5/10 against 7/10). No Ada / Ampere row is the default.
- DENSE_OPT, DSPARK_OPT and MOE_OPT allocate the 5090's big-card scratch and die on a 16 GB card next to COMPACT (79 MiB free). A row's set never turns them on for
  an Ada / Ampere card of 16 GB or less; only an explicit STRATA_INK_SM120_<PART>=1 does.
```

### `9e8e44f9de22` — inkling2 HIP: DFlash 2 reads the shipped pre-quantized mix file (strata-dflash2-q1, variant mix)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/hip/ink_hip_backend.hip.cpp` (+1/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/hip/ink_hip_df2.hip.h` (+123/-14)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/ink_dflash2_mix.h` (+55/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The file Strata downloads next to the model keeps q_proj / o_proj as int8 (NAME.q I8 + NAME.s F32 per row) and gate / up / down as SF4
(NAME.q U8 E2M1 pairs, NAME.s U8 UE4M3 per 16, NAME.o F32). The HIP loader stopped with "SF4 sidecar tensors are not supported on HIP", so every
AMD card ran without a drafter. The int8 matrices load on the existing q8 path; the SF4 ones run natively (k_hdf2_gemv_sf4: a byte-to-two-int8
table, sdot4 on int8 activations, block scales decoded from UE4M3, the same arithmetic as the CUDA kernel), 4.5 bits per weight, so the drafter stays
at about 390 MiB on the card. STRATA_INK_DFLASH2_SF4=bf16 widens them on the host to bf16 with the shared decoder ink_dflash2_mix.h (copied unchanged
from f-owner-mi e46bc2bba) for a comparison. The residency rule counts the loaded size.
```

### `a1df145cea40` — fix(inkling2-cuda): a failed CUDA graph instantiate no longer destroys its graph twice (the stale "invalid argument" that killed the engine after the eager fallback)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_runner.cuh` (+10/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
When cudaGraphInstantiate (pdl::instantiate) fails for lack of memory, the capture code falls back to eager mode. The graph handle was already destroyed right after the
instantiate call, and the failure branch destroyed it a second time: cudaGraphDestroy on the dangling handle returns cudaErrorInvalidValue and leaves it as the last error.
Nothing cleared it after that (the cudaGetLastError() of the branch ran before the second destroy), so the next code that reads the last error took it for its own:
InkTopkScratch::run() ("CUDA error 1 at ink_cuda_backend.cu:1314: invalid argument", the first top-k after the fallback) and ended the engine. It hit the shipped default path too.

Fix, at the five graph sites of ink_runner.cuh (the M-row decode graph, the tree-verify graph, the 5060 Ti prefix+core graph, the batched multi-slot graph and the batched chain-verify graph: GraphKey modes 0, 2, 200+, 10 and 30): gr = nullptr after the first destroy, and
(void)cudaGetLastError() after the failure branch's destroy, so the eager fallback starts with a clean error state. The same pattern was searched for in the whole engine
(a scan for a graph handle destroyed twice on one path): the DSpark / DFlash 2 graphs (ink_dspark.cuh, core/dflash2.cpp), core/mtp.cpp, core/session.cpp, core/verify.cpp,
the HIP backends and hy4 destroy once per path; only these five sites had it.

Test (RTX 3090, SQ-20 v2, DFlash 2, default path, the late-ballast shim of NV-ADA-AMPERE: all but 20 / 60 MiB of the free VRAM taken right before the first stream capture):
the old binary dies at 20 MiB after "Tree CUDA Graph capture unavailable (err=2)", the fixed binary completes (decode 86.7 against 87.4 normal tok/s); with 60 MiB (capture
succeeds) and without the shim both binaries are token-identical with the same speed; FASTQ math on the fixed build 88/90 with no fatal line.
```

**Original commit message**

```
The tip already carries the optional backend hooks (supports_sampled_spec with
the knob-by-knob dspark_spec_step_sampled, prefers_async_prompt_ckpt,
preferred_prefill_cap_with_decoders) from the R9700 and Strix lanes. The
owner's separate hook commit (28adb84f8 / 8acbcfa32) declares the same ideas
under other names and conflicts with the tip in four places, so it is not
applied. The Metal backend now overrides the tip's hooks:

- supports_post_step_prompt_checkpoint -> prefers_async_prompt_ckpt
- dspark_spec_step_sampled takes (temperature, top_k, top_p, min_p, rng_seed,
  rng_offset) and builds its own step seed from the pair
- the prompt-lookup n-gram fallback is removed: DFlash 2 is the only drafter
```

### `950a616192e6` — inkling2: Metal and SYCL DFlash 2 read the shipped pre-quantized file (strata-dflash2-q1, variant mix)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221

  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_dflash2.h` (+42/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/sycl/ink_sy_dflash2.h` (+35/-3)

- Related decisions:

**Original commit message**

```
The owner's two ports only read the 990 MB original model.safetensors (every large
matrix as BF16 under its plain name). The file Strata downloads next to the model
is the 552 MB mix file: q_proj / o_proj as NAME.q (I8) + NAME.s (F32 per row), and
gate / up / down as SF4 (NAME.q U8, NAME.s U8, NAME.o F32), so both loaders stopped
with "missing tensor" and ran without a drafter.

ink_dflash2_mix.h widens the SF4 matrices on the host; the int8 ones are copied
as they are. Both backends then use their existing row-scaled int8 form.
```

```
Measured on the M5 Max (SQ-20, the c1 prompts, 4K / 8K / 16K documents): with the shipped
mix file AND with the original bf16 file the drafter offers drafts and none is accepted
(0 of 64; the engine's own spec check: drafted 40, emitted 62 in 63 steps, same
fingerprint for both files), decode is 11 to 18 percent below plain, and greedy tokens
differ from the no-drafter run on the 8K and 16K documents (also in the exact mode).

kMetalDflash2Available = false keeps the port in the tree but never loads it: plain
decode is the tip's, the DSpark gate is the tip's, and --draft on /
STRATA_INK_DRAFTER=dflash2 print one line ("DFlash 2 is not available on Metal in this
release") and decode without a drafter. The mix loader and the memory rule stay for the
next release.
```

### `5f36afb2a809` — feat(inkling2-cuda): the Tesla P100 (cc 6.0) keeps DFlash 2 by default on a hybrid load (owner: "Turn drafter on at P100")

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_draft_rule.hpp` (+4/-1)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/cuda/ink_cuda_backend.cu` (+1/-0)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_draft_rule_test.cpp` (+16/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
Principle 1b of the drafter rule (below sm_80 a hybrid load never gets the default drafter) gets one exception: compute capability 6.0 exactly, the Tesla P100.
SQ-20 v2 on a P100 16 GB (hybrid, 2313 experts in VRAM), decode tok/s, DFlash 2 against the shipped DSpark and plain: code 29.0 / 21.8 / 12.8, json 29.4 / 22.1, tool 19.7 / 15.3
(prose and chat are lower than plain). The owner wants the drafter on there.

- InkDraftLoadIn::sm60 (set from the card in the CUDA backend: sm_cc == 60), rule 1b becomes `below_sm80 && !sm60 && !full_without`.
- Unchanged on sm_60: principle 1 (a resident model the drafter would push to hybrid, or a tier cut of 10 % or more, is still vetoed), principle 2 (hybrid plain steps from 2048 tokens of
  context), `--draft off` wins. Unchanged elsewhere: sm_61 (GTX 10), sm_70 hybrid and sm_75 (RTX 20) keep the 1b veto.
- Unit test: the P100 case (loaded), the same load on sm_61 / 70 / 75 (vetoed), a 24 % tier cut on the P100 (vetoed), resident -> hybrid (vetoed), --draft off, a resident P100, and the hybrid
  plain-step limit; `ink_draft_rule_test: ok` (g++ -std=c++17).

Smoke on a Tesla P100 16 GB (sm_60, one binary from this commit, SQ-20 v2 + the shipped DFlash 2 sidecar file A (mix, 551,600,700 B, sha256 cf7dbb08...), 2 rounds alternating):
  default start: "[InkDFlash2] DFlash 2 drafter on: ... mix Q8 + SF4 block weights" (2154 experts in VRAM / 918 host, hybrid), no veto line
  decode tok/s, default (DFlash 2) against --draft off: prose 29.8 / 32.1, chat 35.0 / 31.9, json 35.4 / 25.9, code 41.1 / 28.0, count 45.1 / 24.6, tool 36.1 / 26.3, 4K document 20.2 / 19.3
  (the document runs plain steps from 2048 tokens: principle 2); greedy tokens identical to --draft off on prose, json, code, count, doc4k (2/2), chat and tool differ (the batched verify, as on
  every card); --parallel 4 starts, answers 4 requests of 96 tokens (e2e 19.1 tok/s, steady 27.2).
```

### `fb497b985d90` — inkling2(cuda): table row 5090m for the RTX 5090 Laptop GPU (82 SMs, 24 GB): measured, default_on = false

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/include/strata/core/ink_sm120_table.hpp` (+11/-0)
  - `engine/tests/core/ink_sm120_table_test.cpp` (+17/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The laptop card matched no row (the 5090 row wants 160-192 SMs and 30-34 GiB), so it ran the old path. The row matches by name "RTX 5090 Laptop", 78-86 SMs and 20-25 GiB (wide on purpose: an Olares app
sees the HAMi limit nvidia.com/gpumem, 22-24 GiB, as the total memory). Its set is the big set without MOE_OPT and without TC_ATTN (kSm120Laptop5090Fast); exact set empty (= off).

Measured on the card (Olares One, SQ-20 v2, DFlash 2, tip 71292910c, C1 128 new tokens, 2 rounds interleaved against the old path, which is what the card runs by default): decode mean of the six
standard prompts 241 against 201 tok/s, no prompt more than 3% lower; 4K document decode 187 against 118; 4K prefill 6.8k against 4.2k tok/s. The PRO 4000 / 5090 sets (MOE_OPT, TC_ATTN on) lose on json,
count and tool decode and are not used. default_on stays false until the gate has passed on the card (FASTQ, KL against the floor, exact identity, --parallel 1..64):
STRATA_INK_SM120=1 turns the row on. Table test extended (laptop match with and without the HAMi limit, default off, the set, row count 14).
```

### `eae3ca0ffbc8` — inkling2/metal: DFlash 2 is back on for the 16 KiB-aligned SQ-20 pack on an Apple M5 Max (not in the exact mode, plain from 3072 tokens of context)

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_backend.mm` (+10/-3)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_common.h` (+2/-0)
  - `engine/src/inkling2/metal/ink_metal_runner.h` (+24/-2)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The zero acceptance of the first Metal tests was the older SQ-20 v1 pack, not the port: on SQv2-20 (the 16 KiB-aligned file) the drafter accepts
normally with the shipped mix file and with the original bf16 file. The re-enable is gated so that nobody gets a measured slowdown:

- the pack must be the 16 KiB layout (model.page16k); on the older pack, on any other Mac, and in STRATA_INK_M5MAX_EXACT=1 the drafter is not loaded
  and one line says why, whatever --draft says (the exact mode with the drafter changed greedy tokens on 8K / 16K documents)
- from 3072 tokens of context a step is plain, and a prompt that long never feeds the drafter (4K and 8K documents lost 9 to 12 percent decode and 25
  percent prompt speed with it; hint_prompt_len marks the slot)
- the memory estimate matches the measured load (1159 MiB with one slot)

SQv2-20, M5 Max, greedy, 128 new tokens, 3 rounds, default mode, decode tok/s without / with the drafter: prose 57.0 / 55.9 (19 percent of the offered
drafts accepted), chat 57.0 / 81.0, json 56.9 / 135.1, code 56.9 / 131.3, count 56.9 / 115.7, tool 56.9 / 112.7; documents of 4K / 8K / 16K are plain.
Tokens equal the no-drafter run on 5 of 6 prompts (json differs at a near-tie, as the batched verify is not bit-identical to one row);
STRATA_INK_M5MAX_EXACT=1 equals the tip on all 9 prompts.
```

### `cd5fec5d36f6` — inkling2/sycl: the fast Intel Arc set is refused on Battlemage (the exact set runs), decided by the device's architecture

- Date: 2026-11-27

**Original commit message**

```
On a real Arc Pro B70 (the B70 workflow run, all experts in VRAM) STRATA_INK_INTELARC=1 without _EXACT lost the GPU (UR_RESULT_ERROR_DEVICE_LOST) in the
GGUF runs and in the 32-slot run, was not faster on SQv2-20 and returned garbage text from the server; the exact set passed every check there (KLD 0.000000
against off). On Battlemage (oneAPI architecture id intel_gpu_bmg_g21 / bmg_g31 / lnl_m; the name list only when the runtime cannot say) the fast set is
refused with one line ("the fast Intel Arc set is not available on Battlemage in this release; running the exact set") and the exact set runs.
Alchemist keeps the fast set as measured. ink_sy_intelarc_gate_test.cpp tests the decision without a device.
```

### `c159570f64ce` — prefill(hip): the STRATA_PF_SYNC_DEBUG memory-kind query in moe_fused_iq.cu compiles on HIP

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `engine/src/prefill/moe_fused_iq.cu` (+5/-0)

  - `settings-fork-knobs`: ours — FDEC/DOWN_SUB8 и связанные fork-настройки отсутствуют в upstream delta.

**Original commit message**

```
dbg_sync (fd5fc4aa7, #7115) used cudaPointerAttributes / cudaPointerGetAttributes, which the HIP build of strata_pf_own has no mapping for: the Windows HIP Qwen engines (gfx1030, gfx1100, gfx1200) failed to compile on the 0.2.23 freeze tip. On HIP the two calls are now hipPointerAttribute_t / hipPointerGetAttributes (the .type field, as in split/src/hip/fnx_hip.h); the debug switch keeps working there. The CUDA side is the same tokens as before: the change is preprocessor-only.
```

### `82bb6a65cd1b` — inkling2(hip): R9700 multi-slot decode rows (more than 16 rows from two or more slots) run the FP8 trunk and MoE on every layer, the drafter-tapped layers included

  - `engine/src/inkling2/hip/ink_hip_backend.hip.cpp` (+25/-7)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The drafter-tapped layers (5, 11, 17, 23) stay exact for every other step, so a lone request keeps exact taps also on a --parallel 64 server; the FP8 trunk weights of those layers are converted at start-up only
when the server has more than 16 slots, and used only in a qualifying step. p620 (R9700), C64 serve, 64 requests, 3 rounds: 285.0 -> 359.6 tok/s end to end, 359.6 -> 461.7 steady (the default's
tapped layers exact against all layers FP8). Teacher-forced KLD at width 64 on 2047 real tokens: 0.0110 prose / 0.0099 code against 0.0098 / 0.0092 for the default (floor 0.0055 / 0.0047);
EXACT 0.000000. STRATA_INK_R9700_FP8_TAPS=1 still puts the tapped layers in FP8 everywhere.
```

### `e28d9222fa32` — control: custom builds, a person's own .gguf / .strata files of a model the engines run, used in place under their own name

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/serve/server_parts/engines.py` (+2/-2)
  - `terminal/setup.py` (+203/-19)
  - `terminal/strata_control/custom_models.py` (+180/-0)
  - `terminal/strata_control/gguf_preflight.py` (+5/-2)
  - `terminal/strata_control/models.py` (+112/-45)
  - `terminal/strata_control/strata_manifest.py` (+183/-0)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/strata_fixtures.py` (+47/-0)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_custom_models_0223.py` (+604/-0)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_inkling_gguf_import.py` (+2/-2)
  - `terminal/tools/strata_mcp.py` (+44/-9)

- Related decisions:
  - `setup-kat-huihui`: ours — Upstream добавляет Ornith/custom builds, но не KAT/Huihui pins и per-model own_file/own_hf.
  - `setup-upstream-features`: upstream — Берём Ornith, custom-label, hardware refresh, DFlash2 и setup error hint; KAT/Huihui остаются отдельной ours-строкой.
  - `mcp-own-size`: ours — Upstream не заменяет наш per-model own_size lookup.
  - `mcp-upstream-features`: upstream — Берём stale-ok hardware refresh, custom models, Inkling legacy/update metadata; наша own-size строка сохраняется.

**Original commit message**

```
Own model files (a fine-tune, a merge, another maker's quantization) of Qwen3.8-Flash-Next, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, HY-4 Flash and Inkling-2-Mini get
their own run config, strata-<family>-custom-<name>.json, with custom, display_name, base_family, base_model, custom_bytes and custom_source, so an
official install of the same size is never replaced. setup.py gets --custom-label (with --gguf-dir FILE or FOLDER).

- plan_import: the .strata family now comes from Section 0 of the file (arch / model_type; the 4 KiB header at 0x10 and the 16 KiB one at 0x20), not
  from the file name; a GGUF or .strata that is not a published build is reported as a custom build with a suggested name and the closest published size.
- own files are used as they are: only the header, the section table and the tensor layout are checked, never the SHA-256 or byte size, and the file is
  never deleted or renamed. Official downloads keep the strict size and SHA-256 checks.
- HY-4 Flash: a custom .strata is used in place; its Engram and picture files come from beside it, the data folder, or Strata's own checked download.
- fit: the closest published build's rules with the real file size (Inkling-2-Mini, HY-4 Flash); Qwen3.6's context rule uses the closest size.
- remove: a custom build's removal plan keeps every model file; only the run config and Strata's own log and start script go.
- models list, the install tool (custom_label, run_id) and /v1/models (the model name) carry the custom build's name.
```

**Original commit message**

```
The local files screens take .strata and .gguf, and the unsupported message names the four families the engines run. A custom build has an editable
display name, is listed under its family in the version and chat pickers with a Custom badge and its file size, and can be removed from Strata
without touching the files. The host accepts .strata files and the Inkling-2-Mini and HY-4 Flash architectures.
```

### `e22ac39130f9` — control: a .strata whose manifest this Python cannot unpack is read by the installed engine

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/strata_control/strata_manifest.py` (+56/-1)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_custom_models_0223.py` (+62/-1)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
The bundled Python has no zstd, so a compressed Section 0 could not be read. Strata now asks the installed engine (strata-inkling, strata-hy4) for
`--info FILE` (header only, no weights, short timeout) and takes the family from its arch field. The file name and then the size stay only as the
last fallback, with the existing "guessed" message.
```

### `55bb8997e5d3` — control: an engine's --info answer counts only for that engine's own family

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221
- Files:
  - `terminal/strata_control/strata_manifest.py` (+1/-1)
  - `terminal/tests/terminal/test_custom_models_0223.py` (+7/-0)

- Related decisions:
  - No direct matrix decision; accepted as upstream baseline.

**Original commit message**

```
A wrong-family engine that printed an arch line of the other family can no longer decide the family; the next engine is asked, then the name and size guess.
```

### `94e4dbc0775a` — control: no console window when the engine is asked for --info

- Date: 2026-11-27
- Author: Niko1221

**Original commit message**

```
The engine call in strata_manifest passes CREATE_NO_WINDOW like every other engine call in Control, so importing a custom .strata from the app does not flash a console window on Windows. A test checks the flag is passed.
```
